Abstract: Pre-match tactical decision-making in professional football relies heavily on subjective expert analysis and identity-based scouting systems that cannot generalize to unseen teams. This paper presents Sim2Win, a team-agnostic, event-based pre-match tactical recommendation framework that reframes match outcome prediction as a tactical decision-support problem. Using StatsBomb open event data from eleven competitions spanning 178 teams and 1,411 team-match records, Sim2Win constructs five-match rolling tactical profiles, engineers four interpretable tactical feature ratios, clusters team behaviors into eight playstyles via K-Means, and trains thirteen classifiers to estimate win, draw, and loss probabilities from tactical matchup representations.
  The system operates without team names or identity features, enabling generalization to teams never seen during training. A rigorous Leave-One-Competition-Out (LOCO) evaluation demonstrates that Sim2Win achieves a mean ROC-AUC of 0.704 and mean accuracy of 55.4% on completely unseen teams, outperforming ELO, Pi-Rating, and GAP baselines on all 21 ROC-AUC comparisons and 19 of 21 accuracy comparisons. Among all evaluated models, CatBoost achieved the strongest in-distribution performance with 60.90% accuracy.
  These findings suggest that behavioral tactical representations provide transferable predictive signal under distribution shift and offer a viable alternative to identity-dependent football prediction systems.

</details>


### [82] [Meta-Learned Reward Shaping for Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26094)
*Yunpeng Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MeRLa是一个元学习奖励塑形框架，通过在RLHF训练前跨辅助任务学习任务感知的塑形函数，生成复合奖励以提供任务特定的学习信号，在保持策略最优性的同时提升对齐质量。在LLaMA-3-8B上，AlpacaEval 2.0达到90.8%长度控制胜率，MT-Bench得分9.14，训练不稳定性降低41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLHF使用静态、任务无关的奖励模型，导致学习信号稀疏，对齐效果次优。现有方法无法在奖励层面提供任务特定的密集学习信号。

Method: 提出MeRLa框架：在RLHF训练前跨辅助任务元学习一个任务感知的塑形函数Φ(x,y;φ)，生成复合奖励（原始奖励+塑形奖励），并设计联合元目标（任务判别、熵正则化、基于势能的守恒项）来保证策略不变性、收敛稳定性和激励对齐。

Result: 在LLaMA-3-8B上，AlpacaEval 2.0达到90.8%长度控制胜率，MT-Bench得分9.14，相较于PPO、DPO、GRPO、DAPO均有显著提升，训练不稳定性降低41%。且能与过程奖励和基于评分标准的增强奖励兼容。

Conclusion: MeRLa通过元学习奖励塑形有效解决了RLHF中奖励信号稀疏和任务不匹配的问题，提供了理论保证和实证验证，是一种通用且可扩展的对齐增强框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is the standard approach for aligning large language models with human preferences, but its quality is limited by static, task-agnostic reward models. This mismatch leads to sparse learning signals and suboptimal alignment. We introduce MeRLa (Meta-Learned Reward Shaping), a principled framework that meta-learns a task-aware shaping function $Φ(x,y;φ)$ across auxiliary tasks before RLHF training. The learned shaping produces a composite reward that preserves policy optimality while providing task-specific learning signals. Our meta-objective combines task discrimination, entropy regularization, and potential-based conservation for stable convergence. We provide theoretical guarantees for policy invariance, analyze representation drift sensitivity, and formally address incentive misalignment from entropy maximization. Experiments on LLaMA-3-8B across four benchmarks show consistent improvements over PPO, DPO, GRPO, and DAPO, achieving a 90.8% length-controlled win rate on AlpacaEval 2.0 and a score of 9.14 on MT-Bench, with 41% less training instability. MeRLa retains its benefits when combined with process-based and rubric-based enhanced rewards.

</details>


### [83] [Data Fusion and Contrastive Alignment for Unconstrained IR Molecular Structure Elucidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26164)
*Ethan J. Mick,Campbell A. Sweet,Matthias J. Young,Derek T. Anderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非可加聚合算子的MoE解码器改进Transformer模型，用于仅从红外光谱数据中自动推断分子结构，无需预设化学式，Top-K预测准确率提升超10个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子结构解析方法依赖预提供的化学式作为辅助输入，仅能从红外光谱中识别同分异构体，而非进行无约束的完整分子结构预测；Transformer模型在同分异构体识别上表现良好，但在无约束结构解析中可靠性较低且机制不明确。

Method: 1) 在传统编码器-解码器Transformer中引入MoE解码器模块，利用线性顺序统计量和Choquet积分实现非可加聚合；2) 修改Transformer编码器，同样使用非可加算子聚合光谱表示；3) 引入辅助对比对齐损失项。

Result: 与基线IR-only模型相比，Top-K预测准确率提升超过10个百分点。子结构片段分析进一步证实红外光谱编码了绝大部分相关化学信息。

Conclusion: 证明了无需预设化学式、仅从实测红外光谱进行自动化分子结构解析的可行性，显著扩展了AI在分析化学领域的应用前景。

Abstract: Automated molecular structure elucidation from infrared (IR) spectroscopy data has seen significant advancements in recent years, but its broad applicability is limited by a reliance on pre-determined chemical formulas provided as auxiliary model inputs. This limits model predictions to isomer identification rather than full molecular structure prediction. Although transformer models have been shown to identify molecular isomers with high accuracy, their reliability for unconstrained structure elucidation is comparatively low and poorly understood. In this work, we propose and evaluate key modifications to the traditional encoder-decoder transformer. To better address the vast chemical space of the unconstrained problem, we implement a novel Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) decoder module that utilizes non-additive aggregation via linear-order statistics and the Choquet integral. We further modify the transformer to utilize these non-additive operators when aggregating spectral representations as well. Together with an auxiliary contrastive alignment loss term, these enhancements improve Top-K prediction accuracy by over 10 percentage points compared to baseline IR-only models. Through sub-structure fragment analysis of molecular predictions, we further confirm that infrared spectra encode the vast majority of relevant chemical information, implying that the higher performance of isomer-ranking models is largely due to underrepresented or overlapping absorption bands for molecules in the explored chemical space. Ultimately, by demonstrating the efficacy of automated molecular structure elucidation from measured IR spectra, this work serves to significantly broaden the utility of AI in analytical chemistry.

</details>


### [84] [Shared SFT Lessons Across Alignment, Model Organisms, and Toy Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26173)
*Anton de la Fuente,Arthur Conmy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了监督微调（SFT）在三个不同研究领域（对齐训练、模式生物、玩具模型）之间的可迁移性，通过三个具体实验验证了从一个领域学到的SFT教训能否应用于其他领域：1) 将行为泛化教训从对齐训练迁移到玩具模型；2) 将能力保留教训从模式生物迁移到模型规范中间训练；3) 将鲁棒性教训从模式生物迁移到对齐训练。结果显示这些教训在不同领域间可以成功迁移，表明跨领域的技术借鉴有助于推动各领域共同进步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐训练、模式生物和玩具模型通常被视为独立的研究领域，但它们都频繁使用监督微调（SFT）追求相同目标，因此需要检验从一个领域学到的经验教训是否能迁移到其他领域。

Method: 设计了三个迁移实验：1) 将对齐训练中"Teaching Claude Why"的行为泛化教训应用于玩具模型；2) 将模式生物中关于使用脱模型输出会损害能力的教训应用于模型规范中间训练，并测试混合良性同模型数据能否挽回；3) 将模式生物中关于后续良性SFT会擦除对齐行为的鲁棒性教训应用于模型规范中间训练。

Result: 1) 在玩具模型中，基于行为原因的训练比仅基于示例的训练泛化效果更好；2) 混合良性同模型（同策略）数据可以防止脱模型输出带来的大部分能力损害，同时保留目标行为；3) 后续良性SFT会擦除对齐行为而保留能力，表明仅保留能力不足以确保对后续训练的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 跨领域的SFT教训可以成功迁移，不同研究领域之间的技术借鉴能够共同推动各领域进步，建议更多研究者从自身领域之外借鉴技术。

Abstract: Alignment training, model organisms, and toy models are usually treated as separate research areas. But projects in all three frequently use supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to pursue the same underlying goals. When projects share a goal, we should test whether lessons learned from one area transfer to the other areas. We study three such transfers, each taking a lesson developed in one SFT setting and testing it in another. First, we port a lesson about behavior generalization from alignment training into toy models. Training on the reason for a behavior, as in Teaching Claude Why, can make the behavior generalize better than training on examples of the behavior alone. Second, we port a lesson about capability preservation from model organisms into the Model-Spec Midtraining alignment setting. SFT on outputs written by a model other than the student (off-model outputs) can damage capabilities when trained on. Mixing in benign on-model (and on-policy) data into our training can prevent most of this damage while still embedding the target behavior. Third, we port a lesson about robustness from model organisms into the same alignment setting. We find that follow-up benign SFT can erase the alignment behavior while preserving capabilities, showing that capability preservation alone does not ensure robustness to subsequent training. Our work illustrates how porting SFT lessons between different research fields can uplift them all, suggesting more researchers should borrow techniques from outside their own areas.

</details>


### [85] [Dynamic Parameterization Is Not Dynamic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26192)
*Zongfei Li,Yuan-yih Shang,Guozhong Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了动态参数化（输入相关控制器系数）常被错误解读为动态推理或计算节省的证据，实际上混淆了系数变化、冻结模型对系数分配的依赖性和条件执行三个不同属性。作者提出冻结控制器审计（FCA）方法，通过缓存未扰动轨迹上的完整系数张量、禁用控制器并重放冻结模型，量化系数分配依赖性。实验表明：静态逐层配置可保留98.70%-99.43%的性能差距；层身份解释87%-96%的系数方差；FeatureGate和MUDDPythia均执行所有Transformer块且无计算节省。结论：动态参数化≠动态推理，功能动态≠计算节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将输入相关控制器系数视为动态推理或计算效率的证据，但这三种属性（系数变化、冻结模型对系数分配的依赖、条件执行）被混为一谈，缺乏严格的区分和量化方法。

Method: 提出FCA框架：1）沿未扰动轨迹缓存完整系数张量；2）禁用控制器；3）重放冻结模型时进行跨输入重分配、token混洗和静态配置文件估计（基于独立校准集）。通过性能变化量化分配依赖性，避免扰动隐藏状态重新计算控制器的反馈效应。

Result: 76M FeatureGate模型静态逐层配置保留98.70%性能差距，504M模型保留99.43%；层身份解释87%-96%系数方差；FeatureGate执行所有Transformer块，推理速度比Dense慢30.8%；MUDDPythia-1.4B上跨输入重分配和token混洗分别增加NLL 1.9067和2.9637。

Conclusion: 动态参数化本身不证明动态推理，功能动态不证明计算节省。应分别报告：系数变化、冻结模型的功能依赖性、实际执行情况。

Abstract: Input-dependent controller coefficients are often treated as evidence of dynamic inference or computational savings. This interpretation conflates three properties: coefficient variation, dependence of a frozen model on how coefficients are assigned to inputs, and conditional execution. We focus on the second property and formulate a general principle of frozen-controller auditing. We provide one concrete implementation, Frozen-Controller Auditing (FCA), which caches the complete coefficient tensor along an unperturbed trajectory, disables the controller, and replays the frozen model with cross-input reassignment, token shuffling, and static profiles estimated from an independent calibration set. Because the coefficients are cached before any intervention, performance changes under replay measure assignment dependence without feedback from recomputing the controller on perturbed hidden states. Across seven independently trained 76M FeatureGate Transformers and three 504M models, static layerwise profiles retain 98.70% and 99.43% of the Correct-to-GlobalMean performance gap, respectively. Layer identity explains 87% to 96% of the coefficient variance. FeatureGate nevertheless executes every Transformer block, and its measured inference is 30.8% slower than Dense. On the public MUDDPythia-1.4B checkpoint, cross-input reassignment and token shuffling increase NLL by 1.9067 and 2.9637, respectively. These penalties show that the model depends strongly on content-conditioned cross-layer assignment. MUDDPythia also executes every Transformer block. The results show that dynamic parameterization alone does not establish dynamic inference and that functional dynamics do not establish computational savings. Claims about dynamic models should separately report coefficient variation, functional dependence of the frozen model, and actual execution.

</details>


### [86] [Weak-to-Strong On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26246)
*Fangxu Yu,Zinan Lin,Xiaodong Liu,Weijia Xu,Michael Xu,Tianyi Zhou,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出弱到强在策略蒸馏（W2S-OPD），通过从多个弱模型构建代理教师，在logit空间中利用正负模型对比对的差值来提取能力方向，并将其叠加到学生自身的基础模型上，从而让更强的学生模型通过自身rollout上的逐token反向KL散度蒸馏来持续提升。在数学和代码基准上，W2S-OPD优于传统OPD，且能让学生超越领域教师，即使在所有监督源都更弱的情况下也能持续改进学生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在策略蒸馏（OPD）假设教师至少与学生能力相当，要么蒸馏更大模型到更小模型（在模型前沿无更大教师时失效），要么整合多个共享基座的领域专家（需要在学生规模下进行昂贵训练）。为解决这些局限，本文探索如何利用多个弱模型来提升强学生模型。

Method: W2S-OPD框架包含三个关键步骤：(1) 构建正负模型对比对（如后RL专家vs前RL初始化、大vs小基座模型、带正确提示vs错误提示的小基座模型）；(2) 计算logit差值提取能力方向，并叠加到学生的基础模型上，得到与学生分布邻近的代理教师；(3) 学生通过最小化自身rollout上逐token反向KL散度来蒸馏代理教师。三种对比对分别提取：RL注入的技能、规模带来的能力、实例级解题方向。

Result: 在四个数学和三个代码基准上，W2S-OPD优于传统OPD方法；学生能超越领域教师；即使在所有监督源都弱于学生的情况下，模型仍能持续改进。分析表明不同对比对产生不同信号：后RL对比和提示对比强调推理框架，规模对比强调求解过程。

Conclusion: W2S-OPD通过弱模型构建代理教师的方式，有效突破了传统OPD对教师能力必须强于学生的限制，为弱到强泛化提供了一种实用且可扩展的蒸馏框架，尤其适用于模型前沿无更大教师可用的场景。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD), which aligns a student with the teacher's token-level distribution on the student's own rollouts, is an effective paradigm for transferring capabilities across LLMs. Prevailing approaches assume a teacher at least as capable as the student: they either distill a larger model into a smaller one, which fails at the frontier where no larger teacher exists, or consolidate multiple domain experts trained from a shared base, which requires costly training at the student's scale. We introduce Weak-to-Strong On-Policy Distillation (W2S-OPD), a simple yet effective OPD framework that improves the strong student by distilling from multiple weak models. W2S-OPD constructs a proxy teacher in logit space from a contrast pair of a positive and a negative model, both smaller than the student and cheap to obtain. Their logit difference isolates the capability direction, which is added to the student's own base model, yielding a proxy teacher that couples this direction while staying distributionally adjacent to the student. The student then distills it by minimizing the per-token reverse KL on its own rollouts. We instantiate the contrast pair as i) a post-RL expert against its pre-RL initialization, isolating the skill RL instills, ii) a larger against a smaller base model, isolating the capability from scale, and iii) a small base model with correct versus wrong hints, isolating the instance-level direction toward the solution. Across four math and three code benchmarks, W2S-OPD outperforms OPD, enables the student to surpass the domain teacher, and keeps improving the student even when every supervision source is weaker. Analysis shows different contrasts yield distinct signals: the post-RL and hint contrasts emphasize reasoning frameworks, while the scale contrast emphasizes the solving procedure. Our code will be available at https://github.com/Yu-Fangxu/W2S-OPD.

</details>


### [87] [Between Gradient and Natural Gradient: A Continuum of LoRA Initializations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26247)
*Dianze Liu,Farshid Ghezelbash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一低秩适配（ULoRA）框架，将多种LoRA初始化方法纳入一个由谱白化指数和Adam类对角指数组成的二维连续族，发现最优初始化点具有任务依赖性，并设计了无需搜索的ULoRA-Auto变体，在GLUE和GSM8K任务上达到或超过全微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA微调性能严重依赖适配器初始化方案，现有方法（梯度投影法、曲率白化法）看似不同但缺乏统一理解，且最优初始化策略与任务相关，需要系统的设计空间探索。

Method: 提出ULoRA框架，将初始化统一为带谱白化指数和Adam对角指数的预条件梯度族；通过完整学习率扫描探索该二维族；设计ULoRA-Auto根据每层谱统计自适应选择指数。

Result: 调优ULoRA在RoBERTa-base的5个GLUE任务上匹配或超越全微调，在LLaMA-2-7B的GSM8K上达到最强基线水平；ULoRA-Auto无需搜索即可接近调优上限。

Conclusion: LoRA初始化中的曲率预条件应被视为可调维度而非固定设计选择，ULoRA框架提供了系统化的理解和实践方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) fine-tunes large pretrained models at a fraction of the cost of full fine-tuning, but its performance depends strongly on how the adapters are initialized. Recent schemes initialize the adapters from the downstream loss gradient: some project the raw gradient onto its top directions, while others first whiten it with an estimate of the loss curvature. We show that these seemingly distinct methods are points on a single continuum: a two-parameter family of preconditioned gradient initializations, which we call Unified LoRA (ULoRA), governed by a spectral whitening exponent and an Adam-like diagonal exponent. Sweeping this family under a full learning-rate search, we find that no single fixed preconditioning strength dominates: the best operating point is task-dependent and frequently lies strictly inside the family, away from the published endpoints. Treated as an upper bound of this family, a tuned ULoRA configuration matches or exceeds full fine-tuning on all five GLUE tasks with RoBERTa-base and is competitive with the strongest baselines on GSM8K with LLaMA-2-7B. Our deployable, search-free variant, ULoRA-Auto, selects per-layer exponents from measured spectral statistics, approaches this upper bound at no additional search cost, and ranks at or near the top among deployable LoRA methods. Our results show that a principled design space for LoRA initialization and curvature preconditioning should be treated as a tunable dimension rather than a fixed design decision.

</details>


### [88] [Early Verdicts, Better Budgets: Sequential Adaptive Rollout Allocation for Compute-Efficient RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26253)
*Pixel Nomand,Elena Voss,Marcus Hale,Sofia Reyes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RLVR中rollout生成效率低的问题，提出SARA（顺序自适应rollout分配）方法，通过最优停止理论在prompt组尚未完全采样时就判断其有效性，提前放弃饱和组并重新分配预算，在匹配或超越动态采样精度的同时节省22%-67%的rollout开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR中大量采样的prompt产生饱和组（全部正确或全部错误），奖励方差为零导致无策略梯度信号。现有方法要么过度采样后丢弃饱和组（动态采样，额外开销大），要么在采样前预测难度（策略变化时脆弱）。需要一种更高效的rollout分配策略。

Method: 将每组rollout收集建模为预算约束下的最优停止问题。SARA对每个prompt的成功率维护Beta后验分布，计算闭式形式的组有效性预测器，采用双阈值SPRT风格决策规则：确定有效组则提交，确定饱和组则提前放弃，释放的预算重新分配给新prompt，无需额外预测rollout。

Result: 在数学推理和规划任务上，使用1.5B/3B模型在单GPU上测试。SARA匹配动态采样性能（两者均略低于DS oracle），同时比DS少用22%的rollout；将SARA与动态采样组合使用，在比DS少用67%的rollout（接近均匀采样成本）的情况下，精度略高于DS。

Conclusion: SARA证明了放弃可靠性、期望rollout节省、固定预算收益优势，以及有效组收益与GRPO梯度范数之间的联系。提出的方法能够在不牺牲性能的前提下显著减少rollout开销，且与现有方法兼容。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is bottlenecked by rollout generation, yet many sampled prompts produce saturated groups (all responses correct or all incorrect) whose zero reward variance yields no policy-gradient signal. Existing remedies either oversample a larger candidate pool and discard saturated prompts (dynamic sampling), paying heavy extra rollouts, or predict prompt difficulty before sampling, which is fragile under a shifting policy. We observe that a group's effectiveness is usually decided early, within the first few of its rollouts, so spending a full group on an already-decided prompt is wasteful. We cast per-step rollout collection as a budget-constrained sequential allocation (optimal stopping) problem and introduce SARA (Sequential Adaptive Rollout Allocation). SARA maintains a Beta posterior over each prompt's success rate, evaluates a closed-form predictor of group effectiveness, and applies a two-threshold, SPRT-style rule that commits effective groups, abandons saturated ones after a short probe, and reallocates the freed budget to fresh prompts, without any extra prediction rollouts. We prove abandonment reliability, expected rollout savings, fixed-budget yield dominance, and a link between effective-group yield and the GRPO gradient norm. On mathematical reasoning and planning with 1.5B/3B models on a single GPU, SARA matches DPS (both below the DS oracle) while using 22% fewer rollouts than DS; composing SARA with DPS yields the best accuracy, slightly above DS, at 67% fewer rollouts (near-uniform cost).

</details>


### [89] [Top-$k$ Pareto Bandits: Hypervolume Regret for Multi-Objective Slate Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26273)
*Nicolas Gutowski,Fabien Chhel,Alexandre Letard,Sylvain Lamprier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机多目标老虎机问题，提出THV-UCB算法，通过贪婪选择k个臂来近似Pareto前沿，以超体积为指标，证明了无间隙regret界$\tilde{O}(d\sqrt{nkT})$和依赖间隙的polylog界$\tilde{O}(nk^{2.5}/Δ_{\min})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多目标决策中，传统方法旨在找出单一最优臂，但现实中往往需要一组多样化的解来近似Pareto前沿。本文旨在形式化该目标并提供理论保证。

Method: 提出THV-UCB算法，基于臂的边缘超体积贡献的乐观估计进行贪婪选择，利用子模函数最大化中$1-1/e$的近似保证。

Result: 建立了无间隙regret界$\tilde{O}(d\sqrt{nkT})$和依赖间隙的regret界$\tilde{O}(nk^{2.5}/Δ_{\min})$，后者在臂充分分离时呈polylog增长。

Conclusion: 为使用小子集近似Pareto前沿提供了理论支持，适用于各类多目标应用场景。

Abstract: We consider a stochastic multi-objective bandit problem where, at each round, the agent selects a slate of $k$ arms and observes their $d$-dimensional reward vectors under semi-bandit feedback. We do not aim at identifying a single optimal arm; instead, we consider the problem of maintaining a small set of actions that jointly approximate the Pareto frontier. We formalize this objective through the dominated hypervolume induced by the selected subset of arms, and define an $α$-approximate hypervolume regret with respect to the best size-$k$ subset achievable in hindsight, where $α= 1 - 1/e$ reflects the approximation guarantee of greedy maximization for monotone submodular functions. To address this problem, we introduce \textit{THV-UCB}, an optimistic algorithm that selects arms greedily based on optimistic estimates of their marginal hypervolume contributions. We establish a gap-free regret bound $\tilde{O}(d\sqrt{nkT})$ that holds on every instance, together with a gap-dependent bound $\tilde{O}(nk^{2.5}/Δ_{\min})$ that becomes polylogarithmic in $T$ once the arms are sufficiently well separated. Our results provide theoretical support for using small subsets to approximate Pareto fronts in various multi-objective applications.

</details>


### [90] [FloDR: An invertible dimensionality reduction method based on a normalising flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26278)
*Abdallah Baraka,Daniel Probst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FloDR是一种基于可逆标准化流的降维方法，它不仅生成二维嵌入，还保留高维坐标以实现精确逆映射和密度估计，从而支持对嵌入空间进行诊断性可视化（如条件扩散和隐藏对比度），并通过统计检验判断其可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的降维方法（如t-SNE和UMAP）在优化过程中丢弃了高维信息，导致二维嵌入中的聚类距离、空白空间含义及局部结构等特性无法被准确解释，容易产生误导性解读。

Method: FloDR使用可逆标准化流进行降维，前两个输出坐标用于二维嵌入，其余坐标被保留而非丢弃。基于可逆映射可以计算精确逆映射和精确密度，进而生成两种诊断可视化：条件扩散（测量每个嵌入位置在输入单元中未确定的原始数据量）和隐藏对比度（测量两个绘图坐标丢弃了多少关于标签对比的信息）。两种字段都通过预设的统计检验（对比保留数据和自助法置信度）进行可靠性评估。

Result: FloDR能够生成二维嵌入，同时提供精确逆映射和精确密度估计，并支持条件扩散和隐藏对比度两种诊断可视化，这些可视化通过统计检验验证其可靠性，未通过检验的字段会被标记为" refused"。

Conclusion: FloDR通过保留高维信息并利用可逆标准化流，解决了传统降维方法信息丢失导致的可解释性问题，为二维嵌入提供了可靠的诊断工具，有助于防止对嵌入结果的过度解读。

Abstract: It is common for two-dimensional embeddings of high-dimensional data to be read far beyond what they can support. Distances in and between clusters, the meaning behind empty spaces, and the amount of structure hidden at each point are generally invisible in the output of methods such as t-SNE and UMAP. This is because the information that could support the meaning of these properties is discarded during the optimisation process. Here, we present FloDR, a dimensionality reduction method that embeds data through an invertible normalising flow. While FloDR only uses the first two output coordinates to create a two-dimensional embedding, it retains the remaining coordinates rather than discarding them. In addition to the embedding, an exact inverse and an exact density are properties of a trained mapping, which enable diagnostic visualisations that are computed from the exact inverse of the model that drew the layout rather than from an approximate one. Specifically, we draw two fields, the conditional spread, which measures how much of the original data remains undetermined at each embedding position in input units, and the hidden contrast, which measures how much information about a labelled contrast the two plotted coordinates discard. Both fields are rendered with a prespecified test against a held out portion of the input data and a bootstrap confidence. A field that fails the test is reported as refused.

</details>


### [91] [High-Order Markov Blanket Discovery via a k-Order Relaxation of the Faithfulness Assumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26357)
*Loong Kuan Lee,Ragavi Krishnamoorthy,Nico Piatkowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种k阶忠实性假设的松弛方法，并基于此设计了kOMB算法，用于在存在高阶依赖（如XOR关系）和有限样本下忠实性违反时，仍能有效发现变量的马尔可夫边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的马尔可夫边界发现方法普遍依赖忠实性假设，但该假设在XOR、奇偶校验等高阶依赖关系以及有限样本条件下容易被违反，导致方法失效。因此需要一种能应对这些违反情况的MB发现方法。

Method: 提出了一种k阶松弛的忠实性假设，能够刻画k+2个变量之间的奇偶类型关系；并基于此设计了一个概念验证算法kOMB（k阶马尔可夫边界），用于在松弛假设下进行MB发现。

Result: 实验表明，kOMB能够在真实分布和有限样本下出现忠实性违反时，有效地恢复变量的马尔可夫边界。

Conclusion: 通过松弛忠实性假设，kOMB算法能够处理传统方法在高阶依赖和有限样本条件下失效的问题，为更鲁棒的MB发现提供了新的方向。

Abstract: The problem of learning the graphical Markov blanket (MB) of a variable from data has applications in many areas such as structure learning for Bayesian networks and Markov random fields, causal discovery, and feature selection. However, a common assumption most methods make is that the conditional independencies in the distribution imply the same separation in the graphical structure -- also known as the faithfulness assumption. Unfortunately, this assumption can be violated by higher-order dependencies such as XOR and parity-type relations, and -- on finite samples -- by empirical violations that, in extreme cases, even induce spurious dependencies absent from the true distribution. Therefore, in this paper we propose a "k-order" relaxation of the faithfulness assumption that captures parity type relationships between k+2 variables. We then propose a proof of concept algorithm called k-order Markov blanket (kOMB) that uses this relaxation for MB discovery. Finally, we empirically show how kOMB can recover the MB of a variable under both true and empirical violations of faithfulness. Code available at: https://github.com/lklee9/k-order-Markov-blanket

</details>


### [92] [Entity Resolution in Practice: Lessons from a Self-Serve Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26298)
*Kaushik Pavani,Ganga Aluri,Pravin Jadhav,Neeraj Prasad,Kiran Sanka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建并评估了一个自助式实体解析系统，在6个基准数据集（864-500万条记录）上实验，得出三个现有文献未提及的经验：(1) 没有万能匹配算法，建议每个数据集训练多个算法族并自动筛选最优；(2) 精度和召回需要分别修复，精度靠硬规则否决，召回靠多样化候选检索；(3) 单个假正例链接会通过传递性合并导致错误级联，因此跨组合并必须主动重新验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实体解析系统多为针对特定数据集设计的定制方案，缺乏一个通用、自助式的系统，且现有文献未能系统性地总结自助式ER在实际部署中遇到的共性问题。

Method: 构建自助式实体解析系统，在6个规模从864到500万条记录的基准数据集上进行实验评估，通过对比多种匹配算法的表现、分析精度/召回的不同修复策略、以及研究传递性合并带来的错误传播效应，总结出三个关键经验教训。

Result: (1) 单一匹配算法无法在所有数据集上表现最优，推荐多算法族训练+自动bake-off筛选；(2) 精度和召回问题需要分别用硬规则否决和多样化候选检索来解决，而非共享阈值；(3) 传递性合并会导致单个假正例链接级联扩散，跨组合并需要主动重新验证。

Conclusion: 自助式实体解析系统设计应采纳上述三个经验，以帮助从业者避免大量无效实验。

Abstract: We built and evaluated a self-serve entity resolution (ER) system on six benchmarks spanning 864 to 5M records, and three lessons emerged that are absent from existing ER literature. (1) No single matching algorithm wins everywhere - a self-serve pipeline cannot predict its next dataset, so we recommend training several algorithm families per dataset and letting an automatic bake-off pick the winner. (2) Precision and recall need separate fixes, not a shared threshold - precision needs hard rule-based vetoes, recall needs more diverse candidate retrieval. (3) One false-positive link can silently merge unrelated entities - assuming "A matches B" and "B matches C" implies "A matches C" lets a single bad link chain hundreds of records together, so every cross-group merge must be actively re-verified. We hope these lessons save practitioners the months of dead-end experiments that led us to them.

</details>


### [93] [Existence-Field Diffusion Model for Spatial Point Processes with Variable Cardinality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26428)
*Xiaoyin Pan,Christian R. Shelton,Rakshith Mahishi,Chengkuan Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出存在场扩散模型(EFDM)，通过为每个潜在点关联存在变量，实现对空间点过程的联合扩散建模，无需离散跨维度操作即可灵活处理可变基数问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在处理可变基数的空间点过程时，要么解耦基数与空间结构的建模，要么依赖离散的跨维度操作来修改点数，导致生成动态不灵活且不对称。

Method: 提出存在场扩散模型(EFDM)，为每个潜在点引入一个表示其存在程度的存在变量，从而在统一的扩散过程中联合建模空间位置和基数，无需显式的离散跨维度转换。

Result: 在具有可变基数的数据集上实现了更优的建模能力。

Conclusion: EFDM为空间点过程的生成建模提供了一个灵活且通用的框架，有效解决了可变基数场景下的建模难题。

Abstract: We study generative modeling of spatial point processes (SPP), where both the number of points and their spatial configuration are governed by a joint distribution. While diffusion models have achieved strong performance in modeling complex distributions, extending them to variable-cardinality SPP remains challenging. Existing approaches either decouple the modeling of cardinality and spatial structure, or rely on discrete trans-dimensional operations to modify the number of points, resulting in inflexible and asymmetric generative dynamics. We propose the existence-field diffusion model (EFDM) for spatial point processes modeling, where each potential point is associated with an existence variable representing its degree of presence. This enables a unified diffusion process that jointly models both spatial locations and cardinality without requiring explicit discrete transitions. We demonstrate that our approach provides a flexible and general framework for generative modeling of spatial point processes, achieving improved modeling capability on datasets with varying cardinality.

</details>


### [94] [Learning Implicit Causal World Models from Multi-Agent Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26336)
*Jasorsi Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出隐式因果世界模型（ICWM），通过策略方差和顺序后门条件从离线数据中恢复环境动态，无需预定义因果图。在多智能体强化学习中，该方法能解耦统计相关性与因果机制，在协调任务中展现出可解释的因果表征和随干预强度提升的建模精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的多智能体强化学习中，世界模型常将统计相关性与因果机制混淆，导致分布偏移下失效。现有方法依赖预定义因果图，缺乏从离线数据中自主发现因果结构的能力。

Method: 引入隐式因果世界模型，利用策略方差实现顺序后门条件，无需预定义因果图即可从离线演示中恢复环境动态，支持完全和部分可观测性。

Result: 在Two-Door、Navigation和Giveway等协调任务中，模型提供了可解释的因果表征，且模型精度随干预强度增加而提升。

Conclusion: ICWM有效解耦了统计相关性与因果机制，在分布偏移下仍能保持鲁棒性，为多智能体系统的因果建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: In model-based reinforcement learning, world models exist as internal simulators, but their training often conflates statistical correlations with causal mechanisms. This problem is exacerbated in multi-agent systems where physical transitions are intertwined with strategic agent intents, causing world models to fail under distribution shift. We introduce Implicit Causal World Models to recover environmental dynamics from offline demonstrations without requiring pre-defined causal graphs. By incorporating policy variance, we render world models discoverable via the sequential backdoor condition. Evaluations across coordination tasks (Two-Door, Navigation, and Giveway) demonstrate that these models provide interpretable causal representations under both full and partial observability, with model accuracy scaling directly with interventional strength.

</details>


### [95] [Universality and Approximation Rates of Graph Neural Networks with Random Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26699)
*Lukas Gonon,Thilo Meyer-Brandis,Niklas Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带随机节点特征的图神经网络（GNN），证明了结合部分随机节点特征的置换等变神经网络（PENN）可以在概率意义上任意逼近任意可测的置换不变或置换等变函数，并给出了对k次连续可微函数的逼近率上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机节点特征已被理论和实验证明能增强GNN的表达能力，但此前缺乏针对置换等变神经网络（PENN）这一包含许多主流GNN架构的泛化类别的普适性逼近结果。

Method: 理论分析：证明PENN结合部分随机节点特征，在概率意义上可以任意逼近任意可测的置换不变/置换等变函数（针对固定大小的有向图，具有多维节点和边特征）。对于k≥2次连续可微函数，推导了逼近率的上界，将PENN前馈组件的复杂度（层深和非零权重数）与所需逼近精度联系起来。

Result: 建立了PENN结合部分随机节点特征的普适逼近定理，并给出了光滑函数场景下的逼近率上界。

Conclusion: 随机节点特征显著增强了PENN的逼近能力，使其能够逼近任意复杂的置换对称函数，且逼近速率与网络复杂度之间存在定量关系。

Abstract: We investigate message-passing graph neural networks with random node features. Random node features are known to enhance the expressiveness of graph neural networks (GNNs) both theoretically and empirically. Here, we establish a novel universality result focusing on permutation-equivariant neural networks (PENNs), a class of GNNs built from feedforward neural network components that subsumes many prominent GNN architectures. We show that PENNs, combined with partially random node features, can approximate arbitrarily well in probability any measurable permutation-invariant or permutation-equivariant function on directed graphs of fixed size with multidimensional node and edge features. For $k$-times continuously differentiable functions, $k\geq 2$, we also derive upper bounds on the approximation rates, relating the complexity of the feedforward components of a PENN in terms of layer depth and number of nonzero weights to the desired approximation accuracy.

</details>


### [96] [RAGuard: A Layered Defense Framework for Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems Against Data Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26339)
*Pushkal Kumar,Tucker Nielson,Tanish Kolhe,Shubham Zala,Vincent Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAGuard是一种针对RAG系统语料投毒攻击的双层防御方法，包括对抗性微调检索器（第一层）和零知识推理补丁ZKIP（第二层），在5-30%投毒率下将攻击成功率降至0.000，同时保持Recall@5与清洁基线相差不超过0.03。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统依赖外部语料库，容易受到语料投毒攻击（注入恶意文本来操纵检索证据），现有防御方法不足，需要有效的防御机制。

Method: 双层防御框架：1）对抗性微调密集检索器，在合成投毒文档上训练使其降级恶意段落；2）ZKIP零知识推理补丁，通过留一法解码和语义偏移/熵变化评分来过滤检索文档，无需标签、真实答案或模型内部访问。

Result: 在投毒率5-30%的Natural Questions上，ZKIP将攻击成功率降至0.000，Recall@5与清洁基线相差不超过0.03；在BEIR (NFCorpus)上也验证了有效性。防御开销为k+1次生成器调用，可通过批处理和早停降低。

Conclusion: RAGuard能有效防御RAG系统的语料投毒攻击，ZKIP的零知识、黑盒特性使其具有实际部署价值；同时发现关键词保留投毒对BM25等词汇检索器影响较小，界定了威胁模型的边界。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems ground large language models (LLMs) in external corpora, but this reliance exposes them to corpus poisoning: maliciously injected passages that manipulate retrieved evidence. We introduce RAGuard, a layered defense against \emph{factual} corpus-poisoning attacks on RAG pipelines. The first layer adversarially fine-tunes a dense retriever on synthetic poisoned documents (fabricated facts, contradictions, and reasoning traps), teaching it to downrank malicious passages before generation. The second layer, the Zero-Knowledge Inference Patch ZKIP, is a label-free, black-box filter: for each retrieved document, it performs a leave-one-out decode and scores the document by the semantic shift and output-entropy change that its removal induces. ZKIP requires no poison labels, no ground-truth answers, and no access to model internals; it compares the model's own answers under counterfactual contexts. On poisoned Natural Questions at 5--30\% poison ratios, adversarial retriever training alone reduces but does not eliminate attack success, while ZKIP drives the measured attack success rate to 0.000 in every defended configuration, keeping Recall@5 within 0.03 of the clean-corpus baseline. Supervised analyses on both Natural Questions and BEIR (NFCorpus) confirm that the counterfactual signals ZKIP relies on carry learnable poison structure. The defense costs $k{+}1$ generator passes per query ($6\times$ for $k{=}5$); we analyze batching and early-stopping approximations that reduce this overhead. We also show that keyword-preserving poisons leave lexical retrievers such as BM25 essentially unaffected, an observation that delineates the boundary of the threat model. Code, datasets, and evaluation harnesses are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [97] [BayesAME: Bayesian Active Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27023)
*Paula Cordero Encinar,Taylan Cemgil,Arnaud Doucet,Virginia Aglietti,Silvia Chiappa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BayesAME，一个基于序贯贝叶斯框架的方法，能够自动确定用于评估大模型基准性能的核心子集大小。该方法通过建模性能为随机变量，利用历史模型信息构建先验分布，迭代地增量子集直至性能估计波动和不确定性低于用户定义阈值。实验表明，该方法优于现有方法，并证实了非随机子集选择相对于随机选择的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模生成模型评估中，完整基准评估耗时且计算昂贵。现有核心子集方法需要用户指定子集大小，但在性能估计可靠性优先于效率的场景下，需要一种能自动确定合适子集大小的评估方法。

Method: 提出BayesAME框架：1) 将性能建模为随机变量，为共享历史模型性能的每组项目定义潜在能力；2) 通过联合先验分布编码目标模型与历史模型的相似性；3) 利用后验分布推导性能估计器、量化不确定性，并通过信息增益准则选择项目；4) 迭代增量子集直至估计波动和不确定性低于阈值；5) 提出多目标扩展以利用多个目标模型间的性能相关性。

Result: 在多种基准上的实验表明BayesAME一致优于现有方法的序贯适配版本。综合分析解决了学术界近期的质疑，证实非随机核心子集选择优于随机选择。此外，利用连续响应对数似然比传统二值分数显著提升了估计精度。

Conclusion: BayesAME能自动确定核心子集大小，在大模型基准性能估计中表现出色，为非随机子集选择的有效性提供了有力证据，并展示了连续对数似然在性能估计中的优势。

Abstract: Evaluating large generative models across benchmarks is time-consuming and computationally expensive. This drives the need for methods that can estimate full benchmark performance by evaluating models on only a subset of items, known as a coreset. Current literature mostly requires the practitioner to input a coreset size. However, when reliable performance estimation takes priority over efficiency, an evaluation method should also be capable of automatically determining a coreset size that reflects this priority. We introduce BayesAME, a sequential Bayesian framework specifically targeting automatic determination of the coreset size. BayesAME models performance as a random variable by defining a latent ability for each group of items sharing the same historical model performances, with a joint prior distribution encoding the belief that the target model behaves similarly to these historical models. The posterior distribution over these abilities is used to derive performance estimators, quantify performance uncertainty, and select items to add to the coreset via an information-gain criterion. The coreset is iteratively augmented until the performance estimate fluctuation and the performance uncertainty fall below their respective user-defined thresholds. We propose a multi-target extension that captures performance correlations across multiple target models to further reduce the coreset size. Through extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks, we demonstrate that BayesAME consistently outperforms sequential adaptations of existing methods. Crucially, our comprehensive analysis addresses recent skepticism in the literature, establishing that non-random coreset selection is advantageous over random selection. Finally, we highlight that leveraging continuous response log-likelihoods over traditional binary scores significantly enhances estimation accuracy.

</details>


### [98] [Automorphism-Induced Non-Canonicity in Top-k Explanations of Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26344)
*Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Kaizhen Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 图神经网络（GNN）解释器在分子图中因消息传递的置换等变性，无法区分化学等价的官能团（如两个硝基），导致解释结果在对称性下必然任意。当最小有效解释不固定于输入的自同构群时，无法同时满足单值性、最小性和对称性。作者在Lean 4中形式化了一个无参数标准，可判定任意预算下最优报告是否必须分裂轨道，并在21298个实例上验证无误。该问题在常用数据集（如Mutagenicity，93.4%分子有非平凡自同构）中普遍存在，报告轨道而非边可消除任意性，仅增加0.43条额外边和0.11ms开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN解释器在对称输入（如含两个化学等价硝基的分子）上会产生任意性解释——归因分数相等但Top-k边报告却只能选其一，这种任意性源于消息传递的置换等变性这一结构障碍，而非实现疏忽。作者旨在揭示、形式化并量化这一问题，并提出解决方案。

Method: 理论分析：证明当最小有效解释不固定于输入的自同构群时，无法同时满足单值性、最小性和对称性。形式化验证：在Lean 4中实现无参数判定准则，判断解释报告中是否必须分裂轨道。大规模实证：在21298个实例上对比该准则与机械模型等价检查，结果完全一致。数据集统计：量化Mutagenicity（93.4%有非平凡自同构）、MUTAG等数据集中对称性问题的普遍性。

Result: 21298个实例上理论准则与机械验证完全一致，无一例外；Mutagenicity中93.4%分子有非平凡自同构；在常用稀疏度预算下，24.0%含两个可互换硝基的分子（6/25）的解释报告是任意的；MUTAG中模型因输入盲区制造了人工对称性。报告轨道而非边可消除任意性，仅增加0.43条边和0.11ms开销。

Conclusion: GNN解释中因对称性导致的任意性是结构性而非实现性问题，普遍存在于分子图数据集中。作者提供了形式化的判定标准和轻量级解决方案（报告轨道），并指出模型本身的盲区也会人为制造对称性。该问题不能通过简单的参数调整解决，而需要从对称性角度重新设计解释报告方式。

Abstract: A gradient-based GNN explainer given a molecule with two chemically equivalent nitro groups assigns them attribution scores that are equal to the last bit. It cannot do otherwise: message passing is exactly permutation equivariant, so any automorphism of the input leaves every attribution invariant. Yet the standard report, the top-k edges, names one of the two, and which one is settled by the order of an array. We show this is a structural obstruction rather than an implementation slip. When no minimal valid explanation is fixed by the input's automorphism group, no rule can be single-valued, minimal and symmetry-respecting at once. For the exact-k reports used in practice we give a parameter-free criterion, mechanised in Lean 4 with no axiom dependencies, that decides from the graph alone whether every score-optimal report of that size must split an orbit. Across 21298 instance-budget decisions the criterion agrees with a mechanical model-equivalence check without exception, and no severing case we found admitted a neutral alternative. The obstruction is common. Nontrivial automorphisms occur in 93.4% of Mutagenicity, the dataset the seminal explainability papers use, so the measure-zero dismissal of symmetric inputs, sound on the continuous domains it was made for, collapses here. At the sparsity budget those papers report, 24.0% of molecules with two interchangeable nitro groups (6 of 25) surface exactly one of them, every one arbitrary under mechanical verification. A model's blindness also manufactures symmetry: every MUTAG molecule contains atoms chemistry separates and the network provably cannot, and a matched control shows the resolution is set by what the model reads rather than how it is parameterised. Reporting orbits removes the arbitrariness at 0.11 ms and 0.43 extra edges per graph.

</details>


### [99] [From Classification to Regression: Using a Fruitfly to Solve Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27196)
*Shady E. Ahmed,Panos Stinis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 受果蝇感知机制启发，提出一种用分类方法解决回归任务的新型框架，通过有限局部模式库替代全局代理模型，利用相似度匹配和加权重构进行预测，适用于非线性动力系统、数据驱动回归和物理信息学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回归方法依赖复杂全局代理模型，计算和存储成本高。受果蝇感官系统的启发，探索是否可以用有限局部模式的组合来高效逼近非线性输入输出关系，从而降低计算负担并显式控制精度-存储-推理成本的权衡。

Method: 构建一个包含代表性局部模式的有限库；对查询样本，通过度量其与存储模式的相似度，选择匹配模式；将对应响应通过加权重构生成最终预测。针对动力系统采用离线-在线工作流：离线阶段从数据或控制方程中提取模式，在线阶段仅需相似度评估和响应聚合。

Result: 该方法在非线性动力系统、数据驱动回归和物理信息学习任务上均有效，能够降低计算和存储需求，并提供对精度、存储和推理成本之间权衡的显式控制。

Conclusion: 受果蝇启发的分类式回归框架为高效处理科学数据中的非线性关系提供了一种新路径，特别适合输入空间有限且重复的场景，具有计算轻量化和可调节权衡的优势。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to regression tasks using classification which is motivated by the mechanism used by fruitflies to sense their environment. Specifically, we formulate a general framework for learning nonlinear input-output relationships by replacing complex global surrogate models with a finite library of representative local patterns. Since scientific data often occupy limited and recurring regions of the input space, we generate predictions by measuring similarities between a query and stored patterns, then combining their associated responses through weighted reconstruction. We apply this approach to nonlinear dynamical systems, data-driven regression, and physics-informed learning using suitable embeddings and similarity measures. For dynamical systems, our offline-online workflow extracts patterns from data or governing equations during the offline phase, while online prediction requires only similarity evaluation and response aggregation. This structure helps us reduce computational and memory demands while providing explicit control over the trade-off among accuracy, storage, and inference cost.

</details>


### [100] [MetaKoopman: Bayesian Meta-Learning of Koopman Operators for Modeling Structured Dynamics under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26345)
*Mahmoud Selim,Sriharsha Bhat,Karl H. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaKoopman提出了一种贝叶斯元学习框架，通过学习Koopman算子的矩阵正态-逆威沙特先验，实现对非线性动力学的线性潜表示建模，并在分布偏移下提供准确的预测与不确定性量化，在自动驾驶卡车拖车系统的冬季极端场景中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实系统中存在分布偏移，传统的非线性动力学建模与预测方法在鲁棒性和不确定性量化方面不足，难以支持稳健决策。

Method: 提出MetaKoopman框架，采用贝叶斯元学习方法，学习Koopman算子的MNIW先验，通过闭式贝叶斯更新条件化于最近轨迹片段，提供未来状态轨迹的闭式后验预测分布，同时捕捉认知不确定性和偶然不确定性。

Result: 在全尺寸自动驾驶卡车拖车系统的冬季场景（雪、冰、混合摩擦条件）以及模拟控制任务中，MetaKoopman在多步预测精度、不确定性校准和分布偏移鲁棒性方面一致优于先前方法；现场实验验证了其在动态可行运动规划中的有效性，特别是在规避机动和牵引极限操作时。

Conclusion: MetaKoopman通过贝叶斯元学习与Koopman算子结合，有效提升了非线性动力学在分布偏移下的预测与不确定性估计能力，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Modeling and forecasting nonlinear dynamics under distribution shifts is essential for robust decision-making in real-world systems. In this work, we propose MetaKoopman, a Bayesian meta-learning framework for modeling nonlinear dynamics through linear latent representations. MetaKoopman learns a Matrix Normal-Inverse Wishart (MNIW) prior over the Koopman operator, enabling closed-form Bayesian updates conditioned on recent trajectory segments. Moreover, it provides a closed-form posterior predictive distribution over future state trajectories, capturing both epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty in the learned dynamics. We evaluate MetaKoopman on a full-scale autonomous truck and trailer system across a wide range of adverse winter scenarios, including snow, ice, and mixed-friction conditions, as well as in simulated control tasks with diverse distribution shifts. MetaKoopman consistently outperforms prior approaches in multi-step prediction accuracy, uncertainty calibration, and robustness to distributional shifts. Field experiments further demonstrate its effectiveness in dynamically feasible motion planning, particularly during evasive maneuvers and operation at the limits of traction. Project website: https://mahmoud-selim.github.io/MetaKoopman/

</details>


### [101] [Post-Training at the Edge of Detectability: A Game-Theoretic Approach to Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26358)
*Keegan Harris,Brian W. Lee,Ian Waudby-Smith,Philip Amortila,Nika Haghtalab,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个博弈论框架，将KL正则化强化学习微调中的奖励-约束权衡重新解释为序列博弈的均衡，其中智能体最大化累积奖励，监控者检测策略偏离参考策略的程度。该均衡等价于最优正则化系数的KL正则化RL问题，系数可通过凹-凸分数规划原理学习。在Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.2-1B上的实验验证了该方法在持续学习中实现了有竞争力的奖励-保留权衡，并可用于审计API提供商。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KL正则化RL微调中正则化系数通常通过启发式或超参数搜索确定，缺乏 principled 的设置方法，导致训练成本增加或奖励-保留权衡不佳。

Method: 将微调过程建模为一个序列博弈：智能体选择策略最大化累积奖励，监控者观察策略输出并检验与参考策略的偏差。利用凹-凸分数规划，将均衡系数学习问题归约为标准的KL正则化RL目标，实现灵活集成。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.2-1B上的实验表明，该方法在持续学习场景中实现了有竞争力的奖励-保留权衡，并可用于审计开源模型的API提供商。

Conclusion: 博弈论视角为KL正则化RL中的正则化系数提供了统计可解释性，并给出了 principled 的学习方法，有效提升了微调中的奖励-约束平衡性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning is widely used in language model training to improve model performance on a target task while limiting drift from a reference policy. A standard way to balance this trade-off is via a KL-regularized RL objective, although this formulation does not by itself provide a principled way to set the regularization coefficient. In practice, the coefficient is typically chosen heuristically or via hyperparameter search, which can lead to unnecessary overhead in training cost or undesirable reward-retention trade-offs. We instead propose a game-theoretic framework that gives this trade-off an explicit statistical interpretation. Specifically, we study a sequential game in which an agent chooses a policy to maximize cumulative reward while a monitor observes policy outputs over time and tests for deviations from the reference policy. Although not originating from the same perspective, we show that the resulting equilibrium policy can nonetheless be expressed as the solution to a KL-regularized RL problem for an optimal regularization parameter that can be viewed as maximizing reward per unit of statistical distinguishability. Drawing on classical results from concave-convex fractional programming, we provide a principled method for learning this equilibrium coefficient via reduction to the KL-regularized RL objective, thus allowing for flexible integration into standard fine-tuning pipelines. In experiments with Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.2-1B, we demonstrate that our methods result in competitive reward-retention trade-offs in a continual learning setting, and illustrate how our framework may be used to audit API providers serving open-source models.

</details>


### [102] [Parameter-Free Dynamic Regret for Online Convex Optimization under Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27073)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HT-PAder算法，在重尾噪声（仅有限p阶中心矩，1<p≤2）的非平稳在线凸优化中，首次实现了无参数、极小极大的通用动态遗憾界。算法结合重启AdaGrad专家和路径元算法AdaGrad-Hedge，无需先验知识，达到~O(GD√(T(1+P_T/D)) + σD T^{1/p}(1+P_T/D)^{(p-1)/p})的期望动态遗憾，并给出了匹配的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳环境下的在线凸优化（OCO）中，存在重尾噪声（梯度仅有限p阶中心矩）时，如何实现无需先验参数的通用动态遗憾（universal dynamic regret）是一个未解决的公开挑战。现有工作要么依赖矩条件，要么需要已知问题参数。

Method: 提出HT-PAder算法，核心包括：(1) 采用几何级数划分的块长度上的重启AdaGrad专家；(2) 路径元算法AdaGrad-Hedge（无需对元损失施加矩条件）来集成专家。算法完全无参数化。

Result: 期望通用动态遗憾为~O(GD√(T(1+P_T/D)) + σD T^{1/p}(1+P_T/D)^{(p-1)/p})，其中D为域直径，G为Lipschitz常数，σ为噪声水平，P_T为比较器路径长度。当p=2（有限方差）时，这是首个无参数极小极大通用动态遗憾保证。同时给出匹配的下界，证明路径长度指数的最优性。

Conclusion: HT-PAder解决了重尾噪声下非平稳OCO的通用动态遗憾问题，实现了无参数、最优的遗憾界，填补了该领域的关键空白。

Abstract: We study online convex optimization (OCO) in non-stationary environments under heavy-tailed noise, where the stochastic gradient oracle admits only a finite $p$-th central moment for some $p \in (1, 2]$. While static regret is well-understood, achieving universal dynamic regret in a parameter-free manner remains an open challenge. We resolve this by proposing \textbf{HT-PAder}, a parameter-free algorithm combining restarted AdaGrad experts over a geometric pool of block lengths with a pathwise meta-algorithm, \textbf{AdaGrad-Hedge}, which requires no moment conditions on meta-losses. For a domain of diameter $D$, Lipschitz constant $G$, noise level $σ$, and comparator path length $P_T$, HT-PAder achieves an expected universal dynamic regret of \[ \widetilde O\left( GD\sqrt{T(1+P_T/D)} + σD T^{1/p}(1+P_T/D)^{(p-1)/p} \right). \] The algorithm does not require prior knowledge of any of these problem parameters. Even in the special case of finite variance ($p=2$), HT-PAder provides the first parameter-free minimax universal dynamic regret guarantee. We also prove a matching lower bound, establishing the optimality of the path-length exponent.

</details>


### [103] [ClockRoPE: Random Fourier Rotations for Temporal Routine Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26369)
*Yiwen Chen,Joshua Ainslie,Krzysztof Choromanski,Xiang Gao,Su-Lin Wu,Yiping Yuan,Qian Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ClockRoPE，通过将旋转位置编码的频率从固定的log-linear调度改为基于周期性注意力调制函数的傅里叶变换导出的随机傅里叶旋转，从而在序列推荐等具有时间周期性的场景中提升建模能力，并在视频平台生产级生成式检索系统中取得在线A/B测试的持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE的log-linear频率调度仅产生长程注意力衰减，无法建模如时间周期性等更复杂的距离-相关模式，限制了其在序列推荐等领域的应用。

Method: 理论证明任意归一化的连续正定注意力调制函数均可由其自身的傅里叶变换诱导的随机旋转（Random Fourier Rotations）近似；据此提出ClockRoPE，从周期性注意力调制函数中导出旋转频率，用于序列推荐的日常模式建模。

Result: 在线A/B测试中，ClockRoPE在价值参与指标上取得一致性改进，并已成功部署于大型视频分享平台的生产级生成式检索系统。

Conclusion: 基于傅里叶变换的随机旋转方法可有效扩展RoPE的表达能力，使其适配具有周期性时间模式的序列推荐任务，并在实际生产环境中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) has been widely adopted in transformer-based large language models. However, its log-linear frequency schedule, originally designed to produce long-term attention decay, limits its adoption in domains with more complex distance-correlation patterns, such as temporal periodicity in sequential recommendation. We investigate the expressiveness of general query/key rotations and find that any normalized continuous positive-definite attention modulation function can be approximated by random rotations induced by its own Fourier transform, which we term Random Fourier Rotations. Building on this theory, we propose ClockRoPE for routine modeling in sequential recommendation, where rotation frequencies are derived from periodic attention modulation functions. In online A/B tests, ClockRoPE demonstrates consistent improvements in valued engagement metrics, and has been successfully deployed in production-scale generative retrieval system at a major video-sharing platform.

</details>


### [104] [Q-Steer: Action-Value Guidance for Molecular Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26391)
*Xinyu Wang,Jinbo Bi,Minghu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Q-Steer 是一种用于分子语言模型的 rollout 时间动作值引导原语，通过离线训练的冻结前缀动作值评分器 PAVS-Q 估计部分 SMILES 前缀下候选 token 的后续奖励，并将归一化值奖励添加到采样 logits 中，在不改变在线 oracle 预算的情况下，在 PMO23 基准上对两个分子语言模型主干和四种优化器均取得了正向收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Oracle-limited 分子优化仅在完整分子生成后才给予奖励，导致策略优化具有短视性：优化器知道分子好，但不知道是哪些中间动作使其变好，缺乏中间动作的细粒度反馈信号。

Method: 提出 Q-Steer，核心是离线预训练并冻结的前缀动作值评分器 PAVS-Q，该评分器估计在部分 SMILES 前缀下选取候选下一个 token 所能获得的后续奖励，然后将归一化的值奖励加到采样 logits 中作为引导。在线优化器的更新规则和 oracle 调用预算保持不变。

Result: 在 PMO23 基准上使用固定 10,000 次 oracle 调用的在线预算，对两个分子语言模型主干和四种优化器进行全因子实验，Q-Steer 在所有 8 个主干-优化器组合中均提升了平均 valid-unique 分数，宏平均分数提升在 +0.033 到 +0.049 之间，每个组合获得 18-20 个任务胜场。机制对照实验表明动作身份的重要性：前缀广播值接近中性，而打乱动作值则会损害性能。

Conclusion: Q-Steer 是一种可复用的 rollout 时间动作值包装器，在不改变在线 oracle 预算的前提下，能够跨越不同优化器家族和策略主干提升平均分子优化奖励。

Abstract: Oracle-limited molecular optimization gives reward only after a complete molecule is generated, while each rollout requires many local next-token decisions. This delayed-feedback interface makes molecular policy optimization myopic: an optimizer can learn that a molecule was good without knowing which intermediate actions made it good. We introduce Q-Steer, a rollout-time action-value steering primitive for molecular language models. Q-Steer uses an offline-trained and frozen prefix-action value scorer, PAVS-Q, that estimates the downstream reward of taking a candidate next token under a partial SMILES prefix, then adds a normalized value bonus to sampling logits. The optimizer update rule and online oracle budget are unchanged; the claim is fixed-online-oracle performance, not equal total compute. On PMO23 with a fixed 10,000-call online budget, complete factorial studies across two molecular language-model backbones and four optimizers show that Q-Steer improves mean valid-unique score in all eight backbone-optimizer cells, with positive macro mean-score gains between +0.033 and +0.049 and 18-20 task wins per cell. Mechanism controls show that action identity matters: prefix-broadcast values are nearly neutral, while shuffled action values harm performance. These results support Q-Steer as a reusable rollout-time action-value wrapper that improves average molecular optimization reward across optimizer families and policy backbones without changing the online oracle budget.

</details>


### [105] [Flow Map Learning via Nongradient Vector Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26398)
*Mark Goldstein,Anshuk Uppal,Raghav Singhal,Aahlad Puli,Rajesh Ranganath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SGFlow提出了一种无需显式可逆约束和反向传播的流映射学习方法，通过非保守动力学训练模型同时求解ODE和隐式速度，在CIFAR基准上10步采样取得最佳FID，且是唯一具有不动点保证的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一致性模型虽能直接学习ODE轨迹上的流映射，但面临计算挑战：需要模型可逆性或通过迭代模型调用进行反向传播，且无法保证目标流映射是损失函数的解。因此需要一种更高效且理论上可保证的方法。

Method: SGFlow通过非保守动力学训练模型，使其不动点恰好是所需的ODE流映射。该方法绕过了显式可逆性约束和模型迭代的昂贵微分，从零开始同时学习ODE解和隐含的速度场，并采用stop-gradient机制保证动力学收敛到期望流映射。

Result: 在CIFAR图像基准上，SGFlow在10步采样时取得最佳FID；在其他步数下与flow matching、Meanflow和Lagrangian map matching保持竞争力；且是唯一提供基于stop-gradient动力学的不动点保证的方法。

Conclusion: SGFlow提供了一种高效且理论保证的流映射学习方法，尤其在中低步数采样场景下具有优势，为扩散/流模型推理加速提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-based models benefit from simple regression losses, but inference incurs significant overhead because sampling requires integration. Consistency models address this by directly learning the flow maps along the ODE trajectory, opening a design space between one-step and many-step approaches. However, existing methods face computational challenges such as requiring model inverses or backpropagation through iterated model calls, and do not always prove that the desired ODE flow map is a solution to the loss. We introduce SGFlow, an approach for learning flow maps that bypasses explicit invertibility constraints and expensive differentiation through model iteration. SGFlow trains a model to compute both the ODE solutions and the implied velocity from scratch by following non-conservative dynamics with a stationary point at the desired flow map. On the CIFAR image benchmark, no single method attains the best FID at every step count: SGFlow attains the best FID at 10 sampling steps and remains competitive with flow matching, Meanflow, and Lagrangian map matching at other step counts, while being the only one with a proven stationary-point guarantee for its stopgrad-based dynamics.

</details>


### [106] [Examining the Efficacy of Graph Neural Network Message-Passing in Regression Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26404)
*Keith G. Mills,Aedan J. DeFrates,Joong Ho Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图神经网络（GNN）在回归任务中的表现，发现深度卷积GNN（特别是GEN）比注意力机制GNN更有效，而其他经典理论驱动的GNN在回归任务中仍保持竞争力和高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GNN的评估和基准测试主要由分类任务驱动，新提出的消息传递层仅在分类上下文中验证性能，而回归任务被忽视。研究者往往直接将最佳分类GNN以现成方式用于回归问题，缺乏对GNN层在回归任务中效能的系统研究。

Method: 在多种回归场景下（包括排序、误差最小化和洞察提取）系统评估不同GNN层的效能，重点比较深度卷积GNN（如GEN）与注意力机制GNN及其他经典理论驱动GNN的表现。

Result: 深度卷积GNN（尤其是GEN）在回归任务中比注意力机制GNN更有效，同时其他经典理论驱动GNN在回归任务中仍保持竞争力和高效性。

Conclusion: 回归任务应作为GNN评估的独立维度，不同GNN架构在回归中的表现差异明显，深度卷积GNN更适合回归场景。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNN) facilitate effective prediction on graph data such as molecules, media networks and neural network blueprints. GNNs facilitate prediction through message passing techniques which define how information flows from a node to its neighbors. Due to the ubiquity of the graph data type, the development of newer and better GNNs has garnered much interest in the machine learning community.
  However, GNN evaluation and benchmarking is primarily driven by classification tasks. Thus, prospective GNN message passing layers are evaluated on their ability to outperform prior work in classification contexts. In contrast, GNNs are equally capable of performing scalar regression prediction, yet this class of problem is often overlooked when proposing new GNNs while the best classification GNNs are utilized in an a priori or off-the-shelf manner for regression problems. In response, this paper studies the efficacy of GNN layers in a slew of regression contexts from rank ordering, error minimization and insight extraction. Results show that deep convolutional GNNs, particularly GEN, are more effective at these tasks than attention-based GNNs, while other classical, theoretically-inspired GNNs remain competitive and efficient.

</details>


### [107] [SCOUT: Per-Context Reset Curricula for Sparse-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26417)
*Siddharth Aphale,Ayushman Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCOUT是一个在线、与学习器无关的重置控制器，通过为每个上下文提供独立课程来解决稀疏奖励强化学习中全局重置调度的不足，在多个导航和操作任务中优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励强化学习中，从无辅助评估起始的轨迹很少能到达任务后期阶段。重置课程通过从更简单的中间状态（称为支架）开始部分训练轨迹来解决这一问题，但现有方法使用单一的全局调度移除辅助，当不同任务实例（上下文）学习速度不同时可能失败。

Method: SCOUT是一个在线、与学习器无关的重置控制器，为每个上下文提供独立课程。它仅使用二元轨迹成功信号，在持续成功后移除辅助，失败后恢复辅助，并在进展停滞时谨慎测试更难的起始点，不改变奖励、优化器或学习器。

Result: 在六个导航和操作环境中，SCOUT改善了学习效果，并在三个无辅助训练在预算内失败的环境中取得成功。在构建的调度冲突中，每种全局调度都使一个组无法解决，而SCOUT同时解决了两个组。平均成功率可能掩盖失败，因此报告了最不成功组的结果。

Conclusion: 重置课程应在学习进度差异发生的尺度上移除辅助。分组调度在学习差异遵循已知分组时有效，但分组内存在差异时可能失败。SCOUT无需分组标签，在两种情况下都表现稳健。

Abstract: Sparse-reward reinforcement learning often fails because rollouts from the unassisted evaluation start rarely reach later task stages. Reset curricula address this by starting some training rollouts from easier intermediate states, called scaffolds. Such a curriculum faces two decisions: scaffold access, obtaining informative starts, and scaffold allocation, deciding how quickly that assistance is removed. Most prior curricula pace removal on one shared schedule, which can fail when task instances, or contexts, learn at different rates. We introduce SCOUT, an online, learner-agnostic reset controller that gives every context its own curriculum. Using only binary rollout success, SCOUT removes assistance after sustained success, restores it after failure, and cautiously tests a harder start when progress stalls, without changing the reward, optimizer, or learner. A counting construction shows that synchronized global pacing can be insufficient when contexts need conflicting amounts of assisted practice. Across six navigation and manipulation settings, scaffold access improves learning and enables success in three where unassisted training fails within the reported budget. In a constructed pacing conflict, each tested global schedule leaves one group unsolved, while SCOUT solves both. Average success can conceal this failure, so we also report the least successful group. Group-level pacing works when learning differences follow known groups but can fail when they occur within one group. SCOUT needs no group labels and remains consistently strong in both cases. A reset curriculum should remove assistance at the scale where learning progress differs.

</details>


### [108] [DHRCL:Training Code LLMs with Dense Hierarchical Rewards and Curriculum Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26457)
*Shuhang Wang,Ziming Li,Hui Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DHRCL提出了一种面向代码大语言模型的强化学习框架，通过分层密集奖励（语法验证、执行成功、单元测试通过率、AST结构相似性）和自动阶段切换的三阶段课程学习（语法→执行→通过率&结构），结合阶段感知的令牌信用重分配机制，在Qwen3系列模型上取得了优于多种基线方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向代码的强化学习方法通常仅使用稀疏的结果奖励或静态组合异构密集信号，未能有效利用语法有效性、可执行性、功能正确性和结构组织这四种描述不同且逐步依赖的编程能力之间的层次关系。

Method: 提出DHRCL框架，包含：(1) 将反馈分解为语法验证、执行成功、单元测试通过率和AST结构相似性四个层次；(2) 三阶段课程学习（语法→执行→通过率&结构），阶段持续时间基于近期验证趋势自动确定；(3) 阶段感知的概率化令牌信用重分配机制，遵循"巩固到精炼"原则。

Result: 在统一Qwen3-8B和KodCode协议下，DHRCL优于二值奖励、通过率奖励、基于奖励模型和可验证密集奖励等基线方法；在Qwen3-4B、8B、14B上均表现一致优势，且优势随模型容量增大而保持。

Conclusion: DHRCL通过层次化密集奖励与自动课程学习有效提升了代码大语言模型的强化学习效果，且具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a natural post-training paradigm for code-oriented large language models because generated programs can be evaluated through parsing, execution, unit tests, and structural analysis.However, existing methods often rely on sparse outcome rewards or statically combine heterogeneous dense signals, even though syntax validity, executability, functional correctness, and structural organization describe different and progressively dependent programming capabilities. We propose DHRCL, a reinforcement learning framework with Dense Hierarchical Rewards and Curriculum Learning. DHRCL decomposes feedback into syntax validation, execution success, unit-test pass rate, and AST-based structural similarity, and organizes these signals through a three-stage Syntax, Execution, Pass & Structural curriculum. Stage duration is determined automatically from recent validation trends rather than manually specified capability thresholds. We further introduce stage-aware probability-based token credit redistribution. The mechanism follows a consolidation-to-refinement principle: it emphasizes established token patterns during syntax-oriented optimization, applies uniform propagation for non-local execution feedback, and allocates more credit or blame to less-established token decisions during final functional optimization. Under a unified Qwen3-8B and KodCode protocol, the experiments compare DHRCL with binary, pass-rate, reward-model-based, and verifiable dense-reward baselines. We further evaluate DHRCL across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and Qwen3-14B backbones, showing that its advantage remains consistent as model capacity increases.

</details>


### [109] [Neural Architecture Search for Traffic Prediction: A Survey of Methods, Challenges, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26467)
*Truong Giang Vu,Li Yang,Richard W. Pazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了神经架构搜索（NAS）在交通预测中的应用，按搜索策略（梯度方法、进化方法、单次权重共享方法）分类，分析了搜索空间设计、搜索效率与架构质量的权衡，并讨论了计算可扩展性、跨城市泛化、动态图结构等开放挑战及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交通预测深度学习模型依赖手工设计，需要大量专家经验且在不同城市和数据集上泛化能力差，NAS提供了一种自动化搜索架构的替代方案。

Method: 按搜索策略将NAS方法分为三类：梯度方法、进化方法和单次权重共享方法；分析每类方法如何设计搜索空间以覆盖时空交通算子，以及搜索策略如何平衡计算成本与架构质量。

Result: 总结了各类NAS方法在交通预测中的适用性和特点，识别出现有方法在计算可扩展性、跨城市泛化、动态图结构等方面的不足。

Conclusion: NAS为交通预测提供了自动化的架构设计途径，但仍面临大规模路网计算可扩展性、手动搜索空间设计局限、跨城市泛化、动态图结构建模等挑战，未来可探索面向时空基础模型的NAS方向。

Abstract: Traffic prediction is a core task in intelligent transportation systems, supporting applications such as adaptive signal control, route guidance, and ride-hailing dispatch. Deep learning models, including graph convolutional networks, recurrent networks, and Transformers, achieve strong results on standard benchmarks, but their architectures are designed by hand, requiring significant expert effort and producing models that often generalize poorly across cities and datasets. Neural Architecture Search (NAS) offers a systematic alternative to manual design. It automates the search over candidate architectures of deep learning models, finding designs that match the spatial-temporal structure of traffic data without manual trial and error. This survey reviews NAS methods applied to traffic prediction, organized by search strategy: gradient-based methods, evolutionary methods, and one-shot weight-sharing methods. For each category, we analyze how the search space is designed to cover spatial and temporal traffic operators, and how the search strategy balances cost against architecture quality. We also discuss open challenges, computational scalability to large road networks, manual search space design, cross-city generalization, dynamic graph structure, and the open question of NAS for spatial-temporal foundation models, and identify directions for future research.

</details>


### [110] [Learning Dynamic User Personas from Implicit Interaction Streams via Iterative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26473)
*Haifeng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IRIS是一个从隐式交互流中学习动态用户画像的框架，无需显式反馈，通过行为预测驱动的闭环迭代优化画像。在合成数据和真实Reddit AITA数据上，IRIS在决策预测准确率（61.0%）上优于静态画像、纯记忆检索和无个性化基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化方法依赖显式偏好监督（如成对比较或人口属性），限制了其在自然交互场景中的适用性。作者希望从用户的隐式交互流中自动提取行为信号并构建动态用户画像，无需用户提供显式反馈。

Method: IRIS框架通过从日常对话中提取行为信号，利用预测驱动的闭环机制迭代优化画像表示。评估协议包括行为预测、画像稳定性和决策预测三个维度。在合成数据（基于公开自传文本）和真实Reddit AITA数据上验证，画像仅基于作者的历史交互构建。

Result: 在合成数据上，IRIS生成的画像稳定且能区分不同用户，同时揭示了纯记忆方法在面向回忆的指标上的局限性。在Reddit AITA 100位作者数据上，IRIS的决策预测准确率达61.0%，优于静态画像、纯记忆检索和无个性化基线。

Conclusion: 隐式行为建模为LLM个性化提供了一种可扩展的替代方案，无需显式偏好学习，为需要持续演化用户模型的适应性对话系统和具身智能体提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Personalizing large language models (LLMs) to individual users is essential for improving user experience, yet existing approaches typically rely on explicit preference supervision such as pairwise comparisons or demographic attributes, limiting their applicability in natural interaction settings. We propose IRIS, a framework that learns dynamic user personas directly from implicit interaction streams by extracting behavioral signals from everyday conversations and iteratively refining persona representations through a prediction-driven closed loop without requiring explicit feedback. We introduce an evaluation protocol based on behavior prediction, persona stability, and decision prediction. A proof-of-concept study on a synthetic interaction stream derived from public-domain autobiographical text shows that IRIS produces stable personas and distinguishes individual users while revealing limitations of memory-only approaches on recall-oriented metrics. We then validate IRIS on anonymized real-world Reddit r/AmItheAsshole (AITA) data, with personas built solely from each author's historical interactions. Across 100 authors, IRIS achieves the highest decision prediction accuracy among all evaluated methods (61.0%), outperforming static personas, memory-only retrieval, and no-personalization baselines. These results suggest that implicit behavioral modeling provides a scalable alternative to explicit preference learning for personalized LLMs and offers a practical foundation for adaptive conversational systems and embodied agents that require continuously evolving models of their users.

</details>


### [111] [Conformal Changepoint Localization and Root Cause Analysis with Corrupted Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26481)
*Seunghun Yu,Meiyi Zhu,Petar Popovski,Joonhyuk Kang,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出加权置信共形变化点定位（W-CONCH）和加权置信根本原因分析（W-CROC）方法，通过在Huber型污染模型下对可能被污染的观测值进行降权，并利用二阶不确定性信号（如证据深度学习或贝叶斯学习）指导加权机制，在保持覆盖保证的同时显著缩小置信集大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共形变化点定位（CONCH）和共形根本原因分析（CROC）方法虽能在无参数假设下提供统计可靠性保证，但在观测数据被异常值、传感器故障或对抗扰动污染时，产生的置信集会变得过大而信息量不足。

Method: 基于Huber型污染模型，提出W-CONCH和W-CROC方法：利用预训练的二阶分类器不确定性信号（如证据深度学习或贝叶斯学习）对观测值进行加权，权重来自对未知污染数据密度的形式化界限；进一步引入元学习程序，优化可微分的置信集大小替代目标，以学习最优权重。

Result: 在基于图像和真实世界的变化点及根本原因基准测试中，基于不确定性的加权方法在保持目标覆盖率的同时，显著减小了置信集大小。

Conclusion: 通过不确定性感知的加权策略，W-CONCH和W-CROC有效缓解了数据污染对共形推断结果信息量的负面影响，为安全关键系统中的变化点检测和根本原因定位提供了更紧凑且仍具统计保证的解决方案。

Abstract: Detecting when the statistical behavior of an engineered system changes, and identifying which component is responsible, are core problems in the monitoring of telecommunication networks, robotic platforms, security infrastructure, and multi-agent systems. In safety- and mission-critical deployments, such decisions must be accompanied by statistical reliability guarantees rather than by point estimates alone. Conformal changepoint localization (CONCH) and conformal root cause analysis (CROC) meet this need by returning confidence sets that contain the true changepoint, or the true root-cause stream, with a user-specified probability, without parametric assumptions on the data-generating process. In practice, however, observations are frequently corrupted, e.g., by outliers, sensor faults, or adversarial perturbations. While the finite-sample coverage of these procedures is preserved under contamination, the resulting confidence sets can become uninformatively large. Adopting a Huber-type contamination model, this paper proposes weighted CONCH (W-CONCH) and weighted CROC (W-CROC), which downweight observations that are likely to be corrupted with the goal of reducing confidence set size when data may be corrupted. The weighting mechanism, derived from a formal bound on the unknown corrupted data densities, leverages pre-existing second-order classifier-based uncertainty signals, such as those produced by evidential deep learning or Bayesian learning. W-CONCH and W-CROC are further generalized by introducing a meta-learning procedure for the weights that optimizes a differentiable surrogate of the confidence set size. Experiments on image-based and real-world changepoint and root-cause benchmarks show that uncertainty-based weighting substantially reduces confidence set size while maintaining the target coverage.

</details>


### [112] [From Conceptual Hydrologic Models to Conceptually Interpretable Neural Networks: A Snow-Water Mass-Conserving-Perceptron Framework for Discovering Catchment-Scale Precipitation-Storage-Runoff Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26492)
*Yuan-Heng Wang,Hoshin V. Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了质量守恒感知器(MCP)框架，将概念性水文模型重构为物理约束、可解释的神经网络。作者开发了雪水MCP网络，在513个CAMELS-US流域上测试，发现两状态网络相比单状态KGEss从0.82提升至0.89，五状态提升至0.90，增益递减。MCP与LSTM性能相当但参数更少，基于AIC和KGE的选择可识别出平衡精度与复杂度的紧凑有向图表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将概念性水文模型重构为具有物理约束且可解释的神经网络形式，以在保持模型可解释性的同时利用神经网络的表达能力，并系统评估不同状态数量、类型和交互对水文建模性能的影响。

Method: 提出质量守恒感知器(MCP)框架，将两状态SOIL-MCP和SNOWMCP概念模型重新表述为质量守恒神经网络；分析跨节点状态信息共享；构建包含1-5个状态、三种可解释MCP单元的单层网络；使用AIC和KGE进行模型选择。

Result: 在513个CAMELS-US流域上，中位KGEss从单状态0.82提升至两状态0.89、五状态0.90，两状态以上增益递减。MCP与LSTM中位KGEss均为0.90，但MCP参数更少。AIC/KGE选择可识别紧凑的盆地特定有向图。

Conclusion: MCP框架能有效将水文概念模型转化为可解释神经网络，两状态即可捕获大部分信息，未来应测试联合训练多水文响应变量。

Abstract: The Mass-Conserving Perceptron (MCP) establishes a modeling paradigm in which conceptual hydrologic models can be reformulated as physically constrained, conceptually interpretable neural networks. Here, we develop a snow-water MCP network framework and evaluate it across 513 CAMELS-US basins. We first recast a coupled two-state SOIL-MCP and SNOWMCP conceptual model as a mass-conserving neural network and show that the hydrologic-model and neural-network formulations achieve comparable predictive performance. We then examine cross-node state-information sharing within two-state HYDROMCP architectures and evaluate broader single-layer networks constructed from three types of interpretable MCP units with one to five states. Across CONUS, the median KGEss increases from 0.82 for one-state networks to 0.89 for two-state networks and 0.90 for five-state networks, suggesting diminishing aggregate gains beyond two states. Basin-specific MCP and LSTM selection yields the same median KGEss of 0.90, while the selected MCP networks use fewer parameters on average. Complementary AIC- and KGE-based selection identifies compact, basin-specific directed-graph representations that balance predictive accuracy and model complexity. These analyses provide an empirical basis for identifying the numbers, types, and interactions of states needed for hydrologic representation. Future studies should test joint training against multiple hydrologic responses, such as streamflow, snow water equivalent, and groundwater storage.

</details>


### [113] [From Interface to Inference: Eliciting Any-Order Inference from Any-Order Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26504)
*Seunggeun Kim,Jaeyeon Kim,Taekyun Lee,Yuyuan Chen,Yilun Du,Sham Kakade,Sitan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出自回归语言模型缺乏原生的任意顺序推理接口，而掩码扩散模型虽具备任意顺序训练目标，但因位置不确定性导致接口与推理之间存在差距。为此提出两种方法：基于插入的掩码扩散（FlexMDM）和潜在空间掩码扩散（LatentMDM），分别通过放宽固定位置约束和在粗粒度语义段上搜索生成顺序来实现真正的任意顺序推理。实验表明两种方法均能诱导出不同的任意顺序推理行为并提升下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多离散推理任务（如代码生成）本质上是非因果的，程序员会在高层结构和局部细节之间来回切换（即任意顺序推理）。而自回归语言模型缺乏原生的任意顺序接口，需要通过手工设计的机制（如填充、下一编辑预测）来实现非因果能力。因此，作者希望设计出原生支持任意顺序推理的模型。

Method: 提出两种互补方法：(1) 基于插入的掩码扩散（FlexMDM），在FlexMDM基础上通过插入操作放宽固定位置承诺，支持非连续区域的生成；(2) 潜在空间掩码扩散（LatentMDM），将预测转移到更粗粒度的语义段，允许在潜在生成顺序上进行搜索。

Result: 训练了7B参数的FlexMDM用于Python代码生成，以及125M参数的LatentMDM用于GSM8K数学推理。实验表明两种方法都诱导出不同的任意顺序推理行为，并提升了下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 掩码扩散模型的任意顺序训练目标并不自动带来任意顺序推理能力，位置不确定性是关键障碍。通过插入操作和潜在空间搜索两种互补方案可以有效弥合接口-推理差距，实现真正的任意顺序推理。

Abstract: Many discrete reasoning tasks, such as code generation, are inherently non-causal: programmers move between high-level structure and local details, a process we call any-order inference. For autoregressive language models, which lack a native any-order interface, non-causal abilities such as infilling and next-edit prediction require hand-designed mechanisms. Can we instead design models that natively support any-order inference? Masked diffusion models have recently emerged as compelling candidates, as their any-order training objective naturally offers an any-order prediction interface. This interface, however, does not automatically yield any-order inference. We demonstrate that this interface-inference gap stems from positional uncertainty: fixed-canvas, token-level models may know what semantic component should appear without knowing where to place it. In light of this, we propose two complementary approaches: (1) Insertion-based masked diffusion, building on FlexMDM (Kim et al, 2025), relaxes fixed-position commitments via insertions, enabling generation across non-contiguous regions. (2) Latent-space masked diffusion shifts prediction to coarser semantic segments, enabling search over latent generation orders. Empirically, we train a 7B FlexMDM for Python coding and a 125M LatentMDM for GSM8K and show that both approaches induce distinct any-order inference behaviors and improve downstream performance. We release our codebase at https://github.com/SeunggeunKimkr/genuine-any-order.

</details>


### [114] [Collaborative Weighting with Pessimistic Critic for Mitigating Overestimation in Off-Policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26509)
*Gong Gao,Xiao Lai,Ziqi Xie,Guojie Chen,Xianhui Liu,Weidong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CWAC的框架，通过分布性评论家建模回报不确定性，并引入协同加权机制联合重加权TD误差和不确定性，同时采用随机悲观值估计来缓解误差传播，有效提升了连续控制任务中离线策略强化学习算法的稳定性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线策略强化学习算法（如SAC、TD3、DDPG）中，TD学习引入噪声目标导致非平稳优化，贪婪策略更新放大早期估计误差，递归传播造成持续高估偏差和训练不稳定。现有方法通过优先采样或修改价值学习目标来缓解，但常过度强调高不确定性转移，进一步放大偏差。

Method: CWAC框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 分布性评论家（Distributional Critic）建模回报不确定性；(2) 协同加权机制（Collaborative Weighting）联合重加权TD误差和不确定性，从可靠样本中学习并抑制噪声更新；(3) 随机悲观值估计（Stochastic Pessimistic Value Estimation）通过对回报分布采样来缓解策略改进中的误差传播。

Result: 在多种模拟连续控制任务上，CWAC显著提升了性能，且可无缝集成到SAC、TD3、DDPG等现有框架中，额外开销极小。

Conclusion: CWAC通过显式建模价值估计中的预测不确定性，有效缓解了高估偏差和训练不稳定问题，为离线策略强化学习提供了一种统一且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep off-policy reinforcement learning algorithms for continuous control typically rely on neural value function approximation to guide policy improvement. However, temporal-difference (TD) learning introduces noisy targets, resulting in non-stationary optimization, while greedy policy updates amplify early-stage estimation errors. The recursive propagation of such errors leads to persistent overestimation bias and degraded training stability in actor-critic methods. Existing approaches attempt to alleviate this issue via prioritized sampling or modified value learning objectives, but often overemphasize high-uncertainty transitions caused by limited data coverage or bootstrapping errors, thereby further amplifying bias.In this paper, we propose Collaborative Weighting Actor-Critic (CWAC), a unified framework that explicitly accounts for predictive uncertainty in value estimation. CWAC employs distributional critic to model return uncertainty and introduces a collaborative weighting mechanism that jointly reweights TD-errors and uncertainty, enabling robust learning from reliable samples while suppressing noisy updates. In addition, we incorporate a stochastic pessimistic value estimation scheme via sampling from the return distribution, which effectively mitigates error propagation during policy improvement. CWAC can be seamlessly integrated into existing off-policy algorithm frameworks such as SAC, TD3, and DDPG with minimal overhead. Empirical results demonstrate that our proposed method significantly enhances performance across a diverse range of simulated tasks. Our code is publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CWAC-348E.

</details>


### [115] [HiFloat4 Format for End-To-End Reinforcement Learning Post-Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26515)
*Hei Yi Mak,Shadan Golestan,Hoang Le,Mehran Taghian Jazi,Yunke Peng,Yaoyuan Wang,Yao Wang,Junsong Wang,Tianchi Hu,Fengchen He,Guipeng Hu,Tanzila Rahman,Anandharaju Durai Raju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次实现端到端FP4强化学习后训练（前向、反向传播均为4比特精度），发现性能下降的主因是rollout激活量化中的离群值导致大量激活值下溢为零。提出Rollout Residual Quantization (Rollout-ResQ) 轻量级校正机制，结合HiFloat4格式，将FP4 RL与BF16的精度差距从4.9%缩小至1.1%，实现近乎全精度的强化学习训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FP4量化研究多聚焦于预训练和推理阶段，强化学习训练场景中的FP4全精度训练尚未被探索。同时，FP4量化在RL训练中面临严重的精度退化问题，需系统分析其根源并提出有效解决方案。

Method: 1) 系统分析FP4 RL中精度退化的主导因素，发现rollout激活量化中的离群值导致大量激活值在FP4下溢为零；2) 提出Rollout Residual Quantization (Rollout-ResQ)，在FP4 rollout矩阵乘法中添加单个稀疏残差校正项，不增加计算开销；3) 采用HiFloat4 (HiF4) 格式，其三层次分级缩放机制在4比特预算下保持分辨率。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-3B和Qwen2.5-Math-7B上，Rollout-ResQ + HiF4将精度差距从BF16的4.9%降至1.1%；应用于MXFP4时，差距从13.6%缩小至5.3%。

Conclusion: HiF4是实现端到端FP4 RL后训练的有效格式，Rollout-ResQ是缩小与BF16差距的关键激活侧机制，FP4格式选择决定了可恢复精度的上限。

Abstract: We present, to our knowledge, the first end-to-end FP4 RL post-training, in which both the rollout and training policies, including their forward and backward passes, operate at 4-bit precision. A systematic study reveals that the dominant source of degradation in FP4 RL is not training-side quantization error but rollout activation quantization: outliers stretch the dynamic range so far that a large number of activation values underflow to zero under FP4. Counterintuitively, restoring the training policy to higher precision while keeping the rollout in FP4 makes accuracy worse than full FP4 baseline, exposing rollout-training mismatch as the principal failure mode and ruling out standard pretraining-style fixes. We address this with Rollout Residual Quantization (Rollout-ResQ): a single residual correction term constrained to a hardware-friendly sparsity pattern, added only to the FP4 rollout matmul -- a lightweight correction that recovers most of the precision lost to outlier-driven underflow without inflating the rollout's compute footprint. On Qwen2.5-3B and Qwen2.5-Math-7B, Rollout-ResQ paired with the HiFloat4 (HiF4) format -- whose three-level hierarchical scaling preserves resolution under FP4's tight 4-bit budget -- closes the accuracy gap to BF16 from 4.9% to 1.1%, bringing fully quantized FP4 RL within striking distance of full precision. Applied to the open-standard MXFP4, the same recipe narrows the gap from 13.6% to 5.3%, revealing that FP4 format choice is a key factor that determines the ceiling on recoverable accuracy. Together, these results establish HiF4 as the enabling format for end-to-end FP4 RL post-training, and Rollout-ResQ as the activation-side mechanism that makes the gap to BF16 closable.

</details>


### [116] [From Unsupervised Subgroups to Hypothetical State-Intervention Policies: An Evaluation of Selected Subgrouping Methods in Observational Health Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26521)
*Vasundhara Acharya,Bulent Yener*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无监督聚类框架，在不使用暴露、结局或估计治疗效果信息的情况下，基于预处理特征构建可解释亚组，用于预算约束下的政策优先级排序。在PIMA和NHANES数据集上的实验表明，不同聚类方法得到的政策效用相近但优先排序的个体不同，且所有比较在多重假设校正后均不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
