We demonstrate these properties on synthetic benchmarks, where TreeCCA matches or exceeds Deep CCA (2.61 vs.\ 2.43 on Signed Power; 2.93 vs.\ 2.89 on Hermite), and on a sparse benchmark with zero linear cross-view covariance, where TreeCCA recovers the true support with $\text{Precision@}S = 1.00$ at $p=50$ while PMD finds no signal. On the UCI HAR sensor-fusion benchmark, TreeCCA achieves comparable accuracy to Deep CCA at $5\times$ lower cost, while XGBoost gain importances directly validate a physics-motivated hypothesis about the data --- an interpretation not readily available with neural encoders. Across five popular tabular multi-view datasets, TreeMCCA consistently matches or exceeds linear CCA in both nonlinear correlation extraction and downstream classification accuracy.

</details>


### [151] [Lottery Tickets Are Not Deployment Tickets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27031)
*Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在实际部署条件下，稀疏模型（包括彩票票据）能否直接替换密集模型而无需重新配置下游决策逻辑。实验发现，尽管稀疏模型能恢复密集模型的准确率，但它们在行为上仍存在显著差异，会导致7%-10%的决策变更，从而产生重新验证和配置的负担。理论分析表明，即使逐点top-1预测一致，也无法保证固定阈值决策不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中关于稀疏化、压缩和彩票票据如何改变模型行为的结论不一致，且未考虑实际部署中决策逻辑已固定的条件。本文旨在从实际角度评估这些混合发现，研究准确率匹配的稀疏模型能否在不重新配置下游决策逻辑的情况下替换密集模型。

Method: 在部署层面研究生产替换问题，对协议相关的多种部署行为（校准、OOD响应、类别级可靠性、表征和下游策略决策）进行审计，并使用行为兼容性距离总结排除干净准确率后的偏差。进行大量实验对比稀疏候选与密集参考模型的行为差异。

Result: 稀疏候选可恢复密集参考的准确率，但行为上仍存在差异；在固定阈值策略诊断的小差距设置中，彩票票据替换改变了7%-10%的接受-审查决策；彩票票据在多种设置中表现出更低的损坏准确率。

Conclusion: 干净准确率认证存在局限性：与固定密集模型建立兼容性不同于将变更唯一归因于稀疏性或将所有测量偏差视为有害。即使精确的逐点top-1一致也无法保证固定阈值决策不变，操作边界附近的小置信度变化即可产生一阶路由变更。

Abstract: Reports on how sparsification, compression, and lottery tickets change model behavior have been mixed in the prior literature, with beneficial effects observed in some studies and adverse effects in others. Moreover, prior work has not considered actual deployment conditions, where decision logic is already fixed for the incumbent. To assess these mixed findings from a practical standpoint, we study the production-replacement question at the deployment level, namely whether an accuracy-matched lottery ticket or another sparse challenger can replace an incumbent dense model without reconfiguring downstream decision logic. We therefore audit a broad, protocol-specific panel of deployment-relevant behaviors spanning calibration, OOD response, class-level reliability, representations, and downstream policy decisions, and summarize clean-accuracy-excluded deviations with a behavioral-compatibility distance. Across extensive experiments, sparse candidates repeatedly recover dense-reference accuracy yet remain behaviorally different; in several study-band-matched settings, LTs also show lower corruption accuracy. In small-gap settings with fixed-threshold policy diagnostics, lottery-ticket replacement changes 7% to 10% of accept--review decisions. This churn creates precisely the burden that drop-in replacement is meant to avoid: reconfiguring and revalidating downstream decision logic. These findings establish the limits of clean-accuracy certification: Establishing compatibility with a fixed incumbent is distinct from attributing churn uniquely to sparsity or treating every measured deviation as harmful. Our theory explains the routing result: Even exact pointwise top-1 agreement cannot bound fixed-threshold decision changes, and small confidence shifts near the operating boundary can generate first-order routing churn.

</details>


### [152] [CoCaRS: Correlation Calibration-Based Redundancy Suppression for Heterogeneous Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27054)
*Fengming Yu,Haiwei Pan,Kejia Zhang,Chunling Chen,Jian Guan,Baoying Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCaRS方法，通过校准特征去相关来抑制冗余，同时保留结构信息，并自适应调节系数，从而提升异构知识蒸馏性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构知识蒸馏中，直接知识转移受限于师生模型架构差异导致的表征差异；而基于冗余抑制的方法存在均匀去相关削弱有用结构信息、固定系数对师生对和训练阶段敏感的问题。

Method: 提出CoCaRS，包含三个组件：混淆证据估计（CEE）用于捕获可靠的语义关系进行相关估计；强度分配控制（SAC）在去相关过程中保留判别性结构；自适应系数调节（ACR）根据相对损失尺度调节冗余抑制目标的贡献。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-1K上的大量实验验证了CoCaRS在提升蒸馏性能和降低系数设置敏感性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: CoCaRS通过校准冗余抑制策略，有效提升了异构知识蒸馏的效果，并增强了对超参数设置的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) enables a compact student model to learn from a powerful teacher and has become an effective paradigm for model compression. The emergence of diverse model architectures has extended KD from homogeneous to heterogeneous settings. However, differences in architectural inductive biases between the teacher and student models often result in substantial representation discrepancies, limiting the effectiveness of direct knowledge transfer. Recently, redundancy suppression has offered a new perspective on heterogeneous KD by preserving cross-architecture invariance and reducing feature redundancy through decorrelation of teacher-student feature correlations. Nevertheless, this formulation may weaken useful structural information through uniform decorrelation, while a fixed coefficient may make the effective contribution of redundancy suppression sensitive to teacher-student pairs and training stages. To address these problems, Correlation Calibration-based Redundancy Suppression (CoCaRS) is proposed to better retain structural information while suppressing redundancy and reduce sensitivity to coefficient settings across teacher-student pairs and training stages. Specifically, CoCaRS calibrates feature decorrelation through Confusion Evidence Estimation (CEE) and Strength Allocation Control (SAC), which respectively capture reliable semantic relations for correlation estimation and preserve discriminative structure during decorrelation. Adaptive Coefficient Regulation (ACR) further regulates the contribution of the calibrated redundancy suppression objective according to its relative loss scale, reducing sensitivity to coefficient settings. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-1K validate the effectiveness of CoCaRS in improving distillation performance and reducing sensitivity to coefficient settings. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [153] [Single-Beat Cuffless Blood Pressure Estimation Using Ear-PPG and ECG with a Lightweight Hybrid Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27076)
*Kindeep K. Dhatt,Tengyue Wu,Hanbang Hua,Yayun Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级多模态可穿戴框架，融合胸部ECG与耳夹式反射PPG信号，结合混合学习架构（一维CNN提取单拍嵌入 + 30个生理特征 + LightGBM回归），实现单拍级别无袖带血压估计，在受试者独立验证下SBP MAE为4.02 mmHg、DBP MAE为1.79 mmHg，较基线模型降低28.2%的联合MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续无袖带血压监测仍面临运动伪影、生理变异性以及传统脉搏波传导时间（PTT）模型在动态条件下鲁棒性不足的挑战。许多现有方法依赖多秒时间窗口来稳定估计，但在实际监测中信号间歇性损坏时该假设常被违背。

Method: 集成同步胸部心电（ECG）和耳夹式反射光电容积描记（PPG），各配6轴惯性测量单元提供运动上下文；混合学习架构：一维CNN从单拍PPG提取64维嵌入，融合30个生理特征（含PTT统计量和心率变异性），经LightGBM回归实现单拍血压估计。

Result: 多阶段压力协议（n=10）与PulseDB公共数据集上受试者独立验证，30次独立运行下SBP MAE为4.02±0.21 mmHg，DBP MAE为1.79±0.05 mmHg，较基线模型降低28.2%的联合MAE。

Conclusion: 该框架实现无需长时上下文的单拍血压估计，支持资源受限条件下的可穿戴轻量化部署。

Abstract: Continuous cuffless blood pressure (BP) monitoring remains challenging due to motion artifacts, physiological variability, and the limited robustness of conventional pulse transit time (PTT) models under dynamic conditions. Many prior approaches rely on multi-second windows to stabilize estimation, an assumption that is frequently violated during real-world monitoring with intermittent signal corruption. Here, we show that discriminative BP-related information is preserved at the single-beat level and present a lightweight multi-modal wearable framework for continuous BP estimation. The system integrates synchronized chest electrocardiography (ECG) and ear-clip reflectance photoplethysmography, each co-located with a 6-axis inertial measurement unit to provide motion context. We introduce a hybrid learning architecture in which a one-dimensional convolutional neural network extracts a 64-dimensional embedding from individual PPG beats and fuses it with 30 physiology-grounded features, including PTT statistics and heart rate variability, followed by LightGBM regression. The method was evaluated using a multi-phase stress protocol ($n=10$) and the PulseDB public dataset with subject-disjoint validation. Across 30 independent runs, the model achieved mean absolute errors of $4.02 \pm 0.21$~mmHg for systolic BP and $1.79 \pm 0.05$~mmHg for diastolic BP, corresponding to a 28.2\% reduction in combined MAE relative to baseline models. By enabling beat-wise estimation without long temporal context, this framework supports computationally efficient cuffless BP monitoring suitable for wearable deployment under practical resource constraints. The source code for this work is available at https://github.com/SYMBIOX-Lab/BP-wireless.

</details>


### [154] [Equilibrium Training of Energy-Based Models with Parallel Trajectory Tempering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27077)
*Nicolas Béreux,Aurélien Decelle,Cyril Furtlehner,Beatriz Seoane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于并行轨迹退火（PTT）的训练算法，通过在整个学习过程中维持平衡采样，解决了能量模型（EBM）在高度多模态和数据稀缺的科学数据集上训练不稳定、马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛混合差的问题。PTT在计算成本上与持续对比散度相当，可提供热化时间估计、平衡样本和精确的对数似然，在受限玻尔兹曼机上持续优于现有EBM训练方法，在离散表格数据上超越最先进的深度生成模型，实现了EBM的平衡最大似然训练的实际高效方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量模型（EBM）具有可解释性框架，但马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）混合性能差常导致其训练不可靠，限制了在科学数据生成建模中的应用。

Method: 提出并行轨迹退火（Parallel Trajectory Tempering, PTT）训练算法，利用优化路径的连续性在学习过程中维持平衡采样；结合储层采样和自适应优化，计算成本可与持续对比散度（PCD）相当。

Result: 在受限玻尔兹曼机实验上，PTT持续优于现有EBM训练方法；在离散表格数据上，超越最先进的深度生成模型，生成更高质量的样本，对过拟合和有限数据具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PTT使得EBM的平衡最大似然训练变得实用且计算高效，同时能提供热化时间估计、平衡样本和精确对数似然等额外信息而无额外成本。

Abstract: Energy-Based Models (EBMs) provide an interpretable framework for generative modeling of scientific data, but poor Markov Chain Monte Carlo mixing often limits their reliability. We introduce a training algorithm based on Parallel Trajectory Tempering (PTT), which exploits the continuity of the optimization path to maintain equilibrium sampling throughout learning. This enables stable and fast training on highly multimodal and data-scarce scientific datasets. Combined with reservoir sampling and adaptive optimization, PTT has a computational cost comparable to Persistent Contrastive Divergence, making it a practical replacement for standard training methods. It also provides direct estimates of thermalization times, equilibrium samples from trained models, and accurate log-likelihoods at essentially no additional cost. Experiments on Restricted Boltzmann Machines show that PTT consistently outperforms existing EBM training approaches. On discrete tabular data, it also surpasses state-of-the-art deep generative models, yielding higher-quality samples and greater robustness to overfitting and limited data. Our results make equilibrium maximum-likelihood training of EBMs practical and computationally efficient.

</details>


### [155] [Scores Are Not Decisions: Cost-Aware Stopping for Tool Acquisition in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27083)
*Yicheng Feng,Yan Zhang,Yan Cheng,Wei Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAM-DF框架，针对LLM智能体在异构成本下选择工具数量的决策问题，通过训练直接优化停止决策与最优延续之间的离线差距，实现了在五个工具使用领域上的最优性价比，相比全量工具暴露减少37%且任务成功率相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具选择方法（路由器/检索器）只能对候选工具进行相关性排序，但无法确定实际应选择多少个工具。获取工具太少会导致任务信息不足，太多则增加成本、上下文负担和隐私暴露。现有方法未解决异构成本下的工具数量选择问题。

Method: 提出成本感知的边际决策聚焦停止（CAM-DF）框架，在排序后的工具前缀上进行停止决策。训练目标直接优化"当前停止"与"最优继续"之间的离线差距：符号标记决策方向，幅度按风险收益加权每个错误。同时提出紧凑可解释变体CAM-DF-lite。证明该目标与停止目标贝叶斯对齐，并证明纯评分规则在异构成本下是次优的。

Result: 在五个工具使用领域的1,343个任务上评估。在τ-bench Retail上，CAM-DF在所有五种排序来源和两种成本制度下均获得最高收益，超越预测-阈值基线。在异构成本和高成本压力下达到SOTA，在弱排序下增益更大。实际执行中，相比全量访问，CAM-DF暴露给智能体的工具减少37%，同时保持可比较的任务成功率。

Conclusion: CAM-DF系列是一种轻量级预执行插件，能将现有工具排序转化为更低成本的获取决策，无需微调底层LLM。在高成本和异构成本场景下表现最优，且不依赖强工具排序器的质量。

Abstract: As LLM agents increasingly depend on diverse external services such as search engines, databases, and connectors, agent harnesses face a fundamental tool-selection challenge: acquiring too few tools leaves the task under-informed, while too many adds cost, context load, and privacy exposure. Routers and retrievers can rank candidate tools by relevance, but a ranking alone does not determine how many are worth selecting. Existing approaches leave acquisition under heterogeneous costs unaddressed. We formulate this decision as cost-aware marginal decision-focused stopping (CAM-DF) over ranked tool prefixes, with CAM-DF-lite as a compact interpretable variant. We train directly on the offline gap between stopping now and the best continuation: its sign labels the decision, its magnitude weights each error by the payoff at stake. We prove this objective is Bayes-aligned with the stopping target and that score-only rules are suboptimal under heterogeneous costs. We evaluate on 1,343 tasks across five tool-use domains. On $τ$-bench Retail, CAM-DF attains the highest payoff among deployable methods, with gains over a predict-then-threshold baseline across all five ranking sources and two cost regimes. Our approach is state-of-the-art under heterogeneous costs and high cost pressure, with larger gains under weaker rankings. In live execution, CAM-DF exposes the agent to 37\% fewer tools than full access while maintaining comparable task success. The CAM-DF family is a lightweight pre-execution plugin that turns existing tool rankings into lower-cost acquisition decisions without fine-tuning the underlying LLM.

</details>


### [156] [Sky sphere representation in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27092)
*Aleksandr Berdnikov,Yevgeny Liokumovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现约100B规模的开源语言模型在残差流中编码了可解码的夜空地图表示，该表示在高维空间中呈现弯曲的不可约流形结构，在LOO测试中R²分数达65-85%，中位角度误差为12°-21°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大规模语言模型（~100B参数）是否在内部表示中编码了关于夜空天体空间关系的结构化知识，即判断模型是否具有可解码的"心理夜空地图"。

Method: 从开源语言模型的残差流中提取表示，使用留一法（LOO）测试评估表示质量，通过主成分分析（PCA）检测表示是否出现在顶层主成分中，并验证表示不是来自相关平面表示的简单泄露。

Result: 大多数开源模型在残差流中具有可解码的夜空表示；该表示在LOO测试中R²分数达65-85%，中位角度误差为12°-21°；且该表示是弯曲的高维不可约特征流形，并非平面表示的泄露。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型内部存在弯曲的高维不可约特征流形（首次发现），表明模型学习到了关于夜空球面几何的结构化知识表示。

Abstract: We analyze whether language models of size ~100B have a representation of the night sky map that is decodable from their residual stream. We find that most of the considered open-source models do have such a representation, and it often even surfaces to the top principal components on prompts that ask questions like ``what is close to this object in the night sky''. In all but one model this representation showed significant scores in LOO testing, containing up to 65-85% of variance ($R^2$-score) and having median angular error down to $12^\circ-21^\circ$. We verify that our representation is not a simple leak from a correlated flat representation. To our knowledge, this representation is the first example of a curved high-dimensional irreducible feature manifold.
  Codes used in the paper are published at https://github.com/l3erdnik/Decodable-sky

</details>


### [157] [Hierarchical Spatio-Temporal Transformer for Coherent Emergency Department Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27106)
*Filipa Lino,Bárbara Tavares,Carlos Santiago,Cláudia Soares,Manuel Marques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HierSTT，一个基于Transformer的分层预测框架，能够同时且一致地预测医院、区域和国家三个层级的急诊科(ED)就诊需求。通过在葡萄牙81家医院的数据集上实验，该方法相比非分层深度学习基线将WAPE降低了32%，并生成了跨层级接近一致的预测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊科面临不确定的患者需求、季节性激增和非紧急就诊的压力。现有方法通常只在单一层级独立预测ED需求，忽略医院、区域和国家系统之间的层级结构，导致预测不一致（如医院级预测汇总后与区域或国家需求不匹配）。

Method: HierSTT是一个端到端的分层Transformer框架：使用时间融合Transformer（Temporal Fusion Transformer）捕获国家级动态；时空Transformer编码器-解码器模块在高层级预测条件下建模区域和医院需求；通过一致性感知损失函数在训练过程中惩罚跨层级的不一致性。

Result: 在覆盖81家医院、5个地区卫生管理局的葡萄牙全国ED数据集上，HierSTT相比最佳非分层深度学习基线将平均WAPE降低了32%，优于所有经典的分层协调方法，并生成跨层级接近一致的预测。

Conclusion: HierSTT证明了在ED需求预测中显式建模层级结构并联合预测多个层级能够同时提升预测准确性和层级一致性，为医疗系统多层级决策提供了更可靠的预测支持。

Abstract: Emergency Departments (EDs) are critical access points in healthcare systems, yet they face persistent pressure from unpredictable patient demand, seasonal surges, and non-urgent visits. Effective ED planning requires forecasts at multiple decision-making levels: hospitals need local demand estimates for staffing and bed management, regions require forecasts to coordinate healthcare units, and national authorities need system-wide projections for capacity planning. However, most existing approaches forecast ED demand independently at a single level, ignoring the hierarchy linking hospitals, regions, and national systems. This can produce incoherent predictions, where hospital-level forecasts do not aggregate consistently to regional or national demand. We propose HierSTT, a hierarchical Transformer-based framework for coherent multi-level ED forecasting. HierSTT jointly predicts hospital, regional, and national level demand in a single end-to-end model. A Temporal Fusion Transformer captures national dynamics, while spatio-temporal Transformer encoder-decoder modules model regional and hospital demand conditioned on higher-level forecasts. A coherence-aware loss penalizes cross-level inconsistencies during training. We further introduce a nationwide Portuguese ED dataset covering 81 hospitals across 5 regional health administrations, with heterogeneous covariates at each level. Experiments show that HierSTT reduces average WAPE by 32\% relative to the best non-hierarchical deep learning baseline and outperforms all classical hierarchical reconciliation methods, while producing near-coherent predictions across levels. Additional resources associated with this work are available at https://github.com/FilipaLino/HierSTT.

</details>


### [158] [Voronoi Histograms for Adaptive Vectorization of Expected Persistence Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27126)
*Kaifeng Zhang,Kai Ming Ting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Voronoi图的直方图向量化方法用于期望持久性图（EPD），在分离与归一化条件下建立了稳定性边界，并证明了该表示在分类与降维任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久性图（PD）能有效捕捉点云拓扑，但计算复杂度高；现有EPD向量化方法依赖预定义的点变换（如高斯或景观函数），缺乏自适应性。本文旨在探索一种无需显式光滑点变换模型的替代离散化方法。

Method: 提出基于Voronoi图的直方图作为EPD的向量化表示，用自适应分区计数替代光滑函数逼近；在分离与归一化条件下建立稳定性边界，并刻画直方图表示保持Wasserstein尺度变化的条件。

Result: 在具有显著拓扑特征的真实数据集上，该表示在分类与降维任务中表现有效，验证了其与Wasserstein度量的关联和稳定性。

Conclusion: Voronoi直方图作为EPD的向量化方法，在不需要显式光滑点变换模型的前提下，提供了自适应的拓扑特征分布表示，兼具理论保证与实际效用。

Abstract: Persistence Diagram (PD) is known to capture point cloud topology effectively, but its computation has high time complexity. Expected Persistence Diagram (EPD) has been developed to reduce the time cost by studying the topology of multiple subsets of a point cloud and it serves as a distribution of topological features. Existing EPD vectorizations often rely on predefined point transformations, such as Gaussian or landscape functions. We study an alternative discretization based on Voronoi histograms, which trades smooth functional approximation for adaptive partition-based counting. We propose to use Voronoi Diagram-based histogram as the vectorization of EPD, without imposing an explicit smooth point transformation model. Under stated separation and normalization conditions, we establish stability bounds and characterize when the histogram representation preserves Wasserstein-scale variation. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed representation on real-world datasets which have significant topological features for classification and dimensionality reduction tasks.

</details>


### [159] [Minimal Markovization via Stable Quotients in Holonomy-Cover Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27132)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Yongshan Chen,Mahdi Imani,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），提出了holonomy-cover决策过程这一结构化子类，通过构建稳定商（stable quotient）实现了最小马尔可夫充分统计量，并据此设计了Holonomy Memory强化学习框架，实验证明了该方法在记忆压缩与决策性能上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境下，智能体需要维护一个可递归更新的历史统计量以恢复马尔可夫性，但最小化这样的统计量通常是未知的。本文旨在刻画并构建这一最小马尔可夫充分统计量。

Method: 定义holonomy-cover决策过程这一结构化POMDP子类，其中可见动力学是马尔可夫的，且每个可见转移对隐藏模式施加固定置换。构建稳定商（stable quotient）——最粗的保持一步奖励和商后继的观测抽象，证明当前观测与稳定类的配对构成精确的有限马尔可夫状态。在可重置诊断条件下，采用最近原型类推断实现指数衰减误差，并通过校准-重启归约将有限MDP保证迁移到恢复的状态。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能从原始状态恢复出精确压缩的商状态，在决策时仅需三个记忆状态即实现完美配序准确率，匹配商oracle性能并优于非oracle基线。

Conclusion: Holonomy Memory RL框架通过稳定商实现了最小记忆表示，在理论最优性和实际性能上均取得了优异结果，为POMDP中的记忆压缩与高效学习提供了新途径。

Abstract: An agent acting under partial observability must retain a recursively updateable statistic of history that restores the Markov property, but the smallest such statistic is generally unknown. We characterize this minimal Markov sufficient statistic for holonomy-cover decision processes, a structured POMDP class in which the visible dynamics are Markov and every realized visible transition applies a fixed permutation to a hidden mode. In particular, we construct the stable quotient, the coarsest observation-wise abstraction preserving one-step rewards and quotient successors, and prove that the pair of the current observation and stable class forms an exact finite Markov state. When the current class is correctly initialized, exact class tracking requires exactly the minimal memory symbols, in the sense that under reachability and pairwise decision separation at a maximizing observation, no arbitrary finite-memory controller can use fewer. Under resettable diagnostics, nearest-prototype class inference has exponentially decaying error, and a calibrate-then-restart reduction transfers finite-MDP guarantees to the recovered state. The results enable \emph{Holonomy Memory Reinforcement Learning}. It represents memory by the current stable class, updates it through ordered edge transports, identifies local class coordinates when diagnostics are available, and applies a standard finite-MDP RL backbone after synchronization. Experiments recover an exact compression from raw states to quotient states and achieve perfect paired-order accuracy with three decision-time memory states, matching the quotient oracle and outperforming the non-oracle baselines.

</details>


### [160] [Cost-Sensitive Conformal Prediction and Human-in-the-Loop Abstention for Imbalanced High-Stakes Decision Support: A Multi-Domain Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27143)
*Manpreet Singh,Akshatha Srikantha,Shyamal Lakhanpal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了标准边际共形预测（CP）在严重类别不平衡和高风险决策场景下对少数类覆盖不足的问题，通过3,150次实验证明Mondrian（类别条件）CP可将少数类平均覆盖率提升61.7个百分点，且结合成本控制弃权机制可显著降低期望决策成本，为高风险决策系统提供了实用的分布无关、成本感知的不确定性量化指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险决策系统（如信用评分、欺诈检测、医疗和工业安全）面临严重的类别不平衡和非对称错误成本问题，标准边际共形预测虽然能提供整体覆盖保证，但在少数类上覆盖极低（最低仅0.5%），亟需一种能够同时保证少数类覆盖率和控制决策成本的不确定性量化方法。

Method: 在15个真实不平衡表格数据集上，使用7种分类模型、3种概率校准技术和10个随机种子，共计3,150次实验，系统比较了边际CP、Mondrian（类别条件）CP和成本控制弃机机制的性能。

Result: Mondrian CP将少数类平均覆盖率提升了61.7个百分点（p < 1e-80），显著优于边际CP；结合成本控制弃机机制后，在现实人工审查预算下比标准决策边界、基于置信度的拒绝器和风险控制拒绝器都更有效地降低了期望决策成本。

Conclusion: Mondrian CP结合成本控制弃机机制是高风险不平衡场景下部署分布无关、成本感知不确定性量化的有效方案，研究还量化了数据集特定的盈亏平衡阈值，为将模糊实例转交人工专家审查的成本效益提供了实践指导。

Abstract: High-stakes decision systems in credit scoring, fraud detection, healthcare, and industrial safety require reliable uncertainty quantification under severe class imbalance and asymmetric error costs. Standard marginal conformal prediction (CP) provides valid overall coverage guarantees; however, we show that it severely under-covers rare, costly minority classes, with minority-class coverage dropping to as low as 0.5% on certain datasets. To characterize and address this limitation, we conduct a comprehensive benchmark comparing marginal CP, class-conditional (Mondrian) CP, and cost-controlled abstention mechanisms across 15 real-world imbalanced tabular datasets, 7 classification models, 3 probability calibration techniques, and 10 random seeds, resulting in 3,150 experimental runs. Our results show that Mondrian CP restores valid minority-class coverage, achieving an average minority-coverage improvement of 61.7 percentage points over marginal CP (p < 1e-80). Furthermore, combining Mondrian CP with cost-controlled abstention significantly reduces expected decision cost compared with standard decision boundaries, confidence-based rejectors, and risk-controlled rejectors under realistic human review budgets. We further quantify dataset-specific break-even thresholds at which deferring ambiguous instances to human experts becomes cost-effective. These findings provide practical guidance for deploying distribution-free, cost-aware uncertainty quantification in high-stakes decision support systems.

</details>


### [161] [Skillful forecasting of offshore winds from satellite scatterometer constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27152)
*Francesco Pinto,Luca Lanzilao,Paco Lopez Dekker,Angela Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WindCastNet，首个基于卫星散射计观测的离岸风速风向即时预报框架。该框架利用部分卷积长短期记忆网络处理来自欧洲、中国和印度散射计星座的不规则时空观测数据，实现对离岸风场的短临预报。在北海区域评估表明，WindCastNet在1小时和2小时预报提前期上相比HARMONIE MEPS数值天气预报模型分别降低23%和7%的均方根误差，在头三小时内比持续性预报提升9-15%的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离岸风电的日内精确预报对电力系统运行日益重要，但现有数值天气预报（NWP）模型并非针对分钟到小时级提前期优化，而初始条件精度在该时间尺度上主导预报技能。虽然卫星散射计观测已常规同化进入NWP，但从未直接用于预报。因此需要开发一种直接基于卫星观测的短临预报新范式。

Method: WindCastNet采用部分卷积长短期记忆（Partial ConvLSTM）网络，处理来自欧洲（ASCAT）、中国（HSCAT）和印度（OSCAT）散射计星座的微波雷达观测。该方法编码空间观测掩码和观测间隔，利用连续时间表示支持任意提前期的预报，有效应对不规则空间覆盖、异步采样和可变重访时间等挑战。

Result: 在北海区域评估：1）相比HARMONIE MEPS模型，1小时和2小时提前期分别降低RMSE 23%和7%；2）在头三小时内比持续性预报提升9-15%；3）强风条件和空间非均匀流场下预报技能下降。

Conclusion: 卫星散射计星座可以为短时离岸风电预报提供独立且具有竞争力的数据源，开辟了可再生能源预报的新机遇，也可推广到热带气旋即时预报等更广泛的海洋气象应用。

Abstract: Accurate intraday forecasts of offshore wind are becoming increasingly important for power system operation and the integration of growing shares of offshore wind energy. Operational forecasts rely predominantly on numerical weather prediction (NWP), which is not optimized for lead times of minutes to hours, where initial-condition accuracy dominates forecast skill. Although satellite scatterometer observations are routinely assimilated into NWP, they have not previously been used directly for forecasting. Here we present WindCastNet, the first satellite-based nowcasting framework for offshore wind speed and direction, introducing a new paradigm for intraday forecasting that learns from spatiotemporally irregular satellite observations. WindCastNet predicts offshore wind fields from observations acquired by satellite scatterometer constellations. WindCastNet employs a partial convolutional long short-term memory network that exploits microwave radar observations from the European, Chinese, and Indian scatterometers despite their irregular spatial coverage, asynchronous sampling, and variable revisit times. Spatial observation masks and inter-observation intervals are encoded, while a continuous temporal representation enables forecasts at arbitrary lead times. Evaluated over the North Sea, WindCastNet reduces the root-mean-square error by 23% and 7% relative to the HARMONIE MEPS model at lead times of 1 and 2 h, respectively, and outperforms persistence by 9-15% during the first three forecast hours. Forecast skill decreases under strong-wind conditions and spatially non-uniform flow. These results demonstrate that satellite scatterometer constellations can provide an independent and competitive source of short-term offshore wind forecasts, opening new opportunities for renewable energy forecasting but also broader marine weather applications, including tropical cyclone nowcasting.

</details>


### [162] [When Do Learned Diffusion Proposals Help Constraint Solving? A Controlled Study on Continuous Algebraic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27169)
*Quang Bui,Sparsh Roy,Akash Gundimeda,Davin Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了基于学习的候选扩增排序器（MARC）在连续代数约束系统求解中的表现，发现其在数值决策上的优势仅存在于高维且变量弱耦合的特定场景；在真实世界的机器人、定位、优化和代数系统中，经典的多起点随机搜索能解决所有问题，而学习方法的优势区间并不存在。作者提出一个无自由参数的随机多起点成功概率模型，精确复现了实验曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续代数约束求解器擅长找满足约束的值，但面对不可解系统时，只能通过枚举进行结构扩增（增广）。本文动机是：评估学习驱动的候选扩增排序（如MARC）是否真的比随机多起点搜索更具优势，并明确其适用边界。

Method: 将约束系统转化为因子图，用图神经网络扩散去噪器生成赋值，再通过精确计算机代数能量下降进行精炼，最后由符号检查器验证解。MARC在K个候选扩增中选择排序，并与随机多起点搜索（相同精炼预算）对比。作者推导了最佳K次随机多起点成功概率公式：1-(1-q(n))^K，其中q(n)为单次可达概率。

Result: MARC在非平衡非线性菜单准确率上显著优于随机（0.997 vs 0.236，p<10^-70）。但在与同等预算的随机多起点对比中：低维耦合系统两者持平，仅在高维系统学习器才胜出；一旦变量耦合，优势消失。对8个真实世界系统，经典多起点全部解决，无一处于学习有利区间。无参数模型以MAE=0.012精确匹配实验数据。

Conclusion: 学习驱动的候选扩增方法并非普遍优于随机多起点搜索，其优势仅局限在高维且变量弱耦合的人造系统中。对于实际应用中的约束求解问题，经典的多起点随机搜索搭配精炼仍然是最可靠的选择。论文给出了学习提案在求解器中能够改善性能的条件映射。

Abstract: Solving a continuous algebraic constraint system requires two decisions: which values satisfy the constraints, and which structural augmentation renders an unsolvable system solvable. Classical solvers answer the first well and the second only by enumeration. On that discrete decision, a candidate-conditioned repair ranker choosing among K augmentations reaches the exhaustive-search ceiling at a fraction of the calls, outperforming random (0.997 vs 0.236 balanced nonlinear menu accuracy; p < 10^-70; 0.982 +/- 0.006 across seeds) and beating a budget-matched per-candidate probe on accuracy and cost. MARC turns such a system into a factor graph, over which a graph-neural diffusion denoiser proposes assignments, descent on an exact computer-algebra energy polishes them, and an exact symbolic checker certifies solutions. Evaluations of diffusion-based proposals rarely include one control: random multi-start under the same refinement budget. Applied to our system, it sharply curtails what the learned proposal contributes on the value decision. Does it beat random multi-start at choosing satisfying assignments? Only narrowly, in a predictable regime. Across trapped low-dimensional families it ties with random restart, but dominates in high dimension, where random search fails. Once variables couple, the advantage is gone. Since all methods share one polish and one checker, best-of-K random multi-start succeeds with probability exactly 1 - (1 - q(n))^K, where q(n) is single-start reachability; one measured constant, with no free parameters, reproduces the entire curve (mean absolute error 0.012). The favorable regime is not specific to our synthetic families: across eight real-world systems in robotics, positioning, optimization, and algebra, classical multi-start solved all eight, none in the learning-favorable regime. We map the regimes in which learned proposals improve solvers.

</details>


### [163] [Inverse Learning of Latent Risk-Neutral Densities from Irregular Option Quotes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27188)
*Lennon J. Shikhman,Michael Galarnyk,Aadi Dash,Nicholas A. Welsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究期权定价精度与隐含风险中性密度恢复精度之间的差异。通过合成基准（已知真实密度）和NIFTY市场数据基准实验发现：双成分对数正态混合模型在多个误差指标上表现最优，但不同方法各具优势（如DeepONet在分位数和方差误差上更优）。数值条件分析表明定价方向存在大量零空间，不同密度可产生相同价格。测试时自适应可提升DeepONet性能，但混合模型和SVI在按到期日分组的数据上仍更准确。结论支持根据目标选择归纳偏好，不存在普适最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确期权价格并不意味着能够准确恢复潜在的风险中性密度，这一重要区别在以往研究中被忽视。作者希望通过系统性的基准对比来揭示定价精度与密度恢复精度之间的差异，以及不同方法在这两个目标上的表现权衡。

Method: 采用两个互补基准：合成基准（已知真实密度用于潜在评估）和NIFTY市场数据基准（仅测试保留市场价格）。比较多种方法包括双成分对数正态混合模型、DeepONet、quote transformer、SVI等。进行数值条件分析，并采用验证选择的测试时自适应策略。

Result: 双成分对数正态混合模型在合成基准上聚合价格误差、L1误差、Wasserstein距离和固定尾部误差最低。DeepONet在1%分位数和方差误差上比混合模型分别降低39.0%和34.6%；quote transformer在结构误设定的Merton族上L1误差降低16.4%。数值分析显示126个定价方向中95个数值为零，L1距离0.061的两个密度可产生相同价格。测试时自适应使DeepONet的RMSE降低28.3%，但混合模型和SVI仍更准确。

Conclusion: 不存在普适最优方法，应根据目标选择具有相应归纳偏好的方法。定价精度与密度恢复精度是两个不同目标，需要分别评估和优化。

Abstract: Accurate option prices do not imply accurate recovery of the latent risk-neutral density. We study this distinction with two complementary benchmarks. A controlled benchmark exposes simulator-truth densities for latent evaluation, while a chronological NIFTY benchmark tests only held-out market prices. A two-component lognormal mixture has the lowest aggregate price, $L^1$, Wasserstein, and fixed-tail errors on the synthetic benchmark. Learned operators retain narrower strengths: DeepONet reduces 1% quantile and variance error by 39.0% and 34.6% relative to the mixture, and a quote transformer reduces $L^1$ by 16.4% on the structurally misspecified Merton family. A numerical conditioning analysis explains why these rankings can differ: after enforcing mass and forward constraints, 95 of 126 pricing directions are numerically null, and two densities separated by $L^1 = 0.061$ produce identical prices on the covered strikes. On 524 held-out NIFTY calls, validation-selected test-time adaptation reduces DeepONet RMSE by 28.3%, but per-expiry mixture and SVI fits remain much more accurate. The evidence supports target-dependent inductive bias, not a universal winner.

</details>


### [164] [Do You Really Need to Pretrain Q-Functions for Online RL Fine-Tuning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27203)
*Perry Dong,Ron Polonsky,Dorsa Sadigh,Chelsea Fin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究基于价值函数的强化学习中的Q函数预训练问题，发现简单的Q函数预训练对在线微调帮助有限，原因在于预训练Q函数与在线微调收敛目标之间存在根本性不匹配。为此提出集成策略初始化方法（IPE），通过训练多个多样化策略并利用其混合轨迹来引导在线RL的Q函数学习，在连续控制基准上取得1.26倍改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于价值的强化学习中，预训练-微调范式下Q函数是否应该预训练？直觉认为应该，但近期研究表明随机初始化Q函数也能取得高性能。本文旨在系统研究Q函数预训练的实际效用及其局限性背后的原因。

Method: 提出初始化策略集成（IPE）方法：训练多个多样化策略，收集它们的混合轨迹，利用这些数据在在线RL中引导Q函数学习，从而弥合预训练与微调之间的不匹配。

Result: 在连续控制基准任务上，IPE相比朴素Q函数预训练取得平均1.26倍的微调性能提升。

Conclusion: 朴素Q函数预训练效果有限源于预训练与在线微调目标之间的不匹配；IPE通过多策略集成初始化有效缓解了这一问题，显著提升了微调性能。

Abstract: Pre-training followed by fine-tuning has become the dominant recipe for learning performant policies, and in value-based reinforcement learning (RL) this raises a natural question: given a pretrained policy, should the Q-function be pretrained on offline data too? Conventional wisdom suggests it should, but recent results show that online RL with a randomly-initialized Q-function can result in highly performant and reliable policies without needing to pretrain the Q-function. In this paper, we systematically study whether pretraining the Q-function actually helps when fine-tuning on top of a pretrained base policy. We find, surprisingly, that naive Q-function pretraining often provides little benefit over random initialization. We show this stems from a fundamental mismatch: the Q-function learned during pretraining targets the pretrained policy's Q-function, not the Q-function that online fine-tuning converges to, and this gap persists even after offline value maximization. Motivated by this finding, we propose Initialization via Policy Ensemble (IPE), a simple method that trains multiple diverse policies and uses their pooled rollouts to bootstrap the Q-function learning in online RL. Across a suite of challenging continuous control benchmarks, IPE yields an average 1.26x improvement in fine-tuning performance over naive Q-function pre-training.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Reversal of a flat plate into its wake: a minimal model for wake capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26260)
*Dirk de Boer,Abel-John Buchner*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过平板往复平移运动实验，研究了扑翼运动中翼-尾迹相互作用产生额外阻力的机理。发现反转前运动距离是关键参数，影响峰值阻力大小和时序；起始涡环的脱离与重新形成导致双峰阻力特征；尾迹中流向速度的空间衰减与阻力时间衰减直接相关；将起始涡简化为点涡可揭示涡环位置、形状和环量对翼-尾迹相互作用时空尺度的影响，其标度关系可用四次方根与线性组合描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管翼-尾迹相互作用的存在已被认知，但对其底层机制和标度规律的基础研究仍十分有限，特别是对实测力与准稳态近似之间差异的解释尚不充分。

Method: 采用平板在前进-后退平移运动反转阶段的流动实验，在昆虫飞行相关雷诺数下研究，通过测量额外阻力并结合流场观测和点涡简化模型进行分析。

Result: 反转前运动距离是控制翼-尾迹相互作用的关键参数；起始涡环的脱离和重新形成产生双峰阻力特征；尾迹流场速度的空间衰减直接对应阻力时间衰减；点涡模型揭示标度关系为四次方根与线性组合。

Conclusion: 翼-尾迹相互作用的时空标度主要由涡环位置、形状和环量决定，反转前运动距离的影响可用组合标度律描述。

Abstract: In reciprocating flapping-like motions, wing-wake interaction plays a crucial role in fluid force generation. While this effect's existence has been acknowledged, particularly in explaining discrepancies between measured forces and quasi-steady approximations, fundamental research on the mechanism underlying this interaction and its scaling remains limited. To address this, we investigate the excess drag force, relative to quasi-steady estimates, acting on a flat plate during the reversal phase of a forward and back translational motion. The flow produced by this motion, studied at insect-flight-relevant Reynolds numbers, serves as a simplified analogue to biological flapping. We demonstrate that interaction with pre-existing wake flow indeed generates excess drag. The main parameter governing this interaction is the distance travelled before reversal, which influences both magnitude and temporal dynamics of the peak drag. We link our observations to optimal vortex formation, as the time trace of the additional drag during reversal is qualitatively altered by the detachment of the starting vortex ring: vortex detachment and re-formation lead to two distinct wake-force peaks. Furthermore, as the pre-reversal distance traversed increases, the wake interaction force post-reversal decays more slowly. Flow observations reveal a similar spatial decay of the streamwise velocity in the wake at the moment of reversal, suggesting a direct link. Representing the starting vortex as a point vortex indicates that the wake's spatial scaling, and commensurately the temporal scaling of the wing-wake interaction effect, is primarily governed by the vortex ring position, shape, and circulation. This simplification reveals a dependence on the pre-reversal translation distance that can be described by a combined fourth-root and linear scaling.

</details>


### [166] [Thermal convection in 1, 2, 3 and 4 dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26372)
*Ambrish Pandey,Harshit Tiwari,Katepalli R. Sreenivasan*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文通过直接数值模拟研究了对流维度（1D、2D、3D、4D）对流动特性的影响，重点关注达到稳态所需的瞬态时间以及所谓的终极状态的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究对流维度（从一维到四维）对流动特性的影响，特别是理解维度如何影响瞬态时间和终极状态。

Method: 采用直接数值模拟方法，系统比较了1D、2D、3D和4D对流系统的流动特性。

Result: 揭示了维度对瞬态时间和终极状态的影响规律，通过跨维度比较总结出一些一般性原理。

Conclusion: 对流维度显著影响流动特性，高维对流系统对瞬态行为和终极状态的预测具有重要启示。

Abstract: We study by means of direct numerical simulations the influence of the dimensionality of convection on flow properties. We call attention to a few general principles from considering in totality the results from 1D, 2D, 3D and 4D. In particular, we explore two practical aspects: (1) The transient time, or the amount of time it takes for the flow to reach the steady state; and (2) possible implications for the so-called ultimate state.

</details>


### [167] [Extracting informative vortical structures of turbulent wake-extreme vortex gust interactions with machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26683)
*Ryo Koshikawa,Kai Fukami*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文利用卷积信息论学习方法，从极端涡-翼型相互作用的涡流快照中，基于对未来目标变量（如升力系数、尺度依赖能量传递）的贡献，分解出信息性分量与残差分量，以提取因果重要的涡结构。研究发现，不同目标变量对应的重要结构在涡撞击前后呈现差异性和相似性，揭示了瞬态气动机制间信息结构的类比关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从极端涡-翼型相互作用的复杂瞬态多尺度流动中，提取因果重要的涡结构，以理解不同瞬态气动机制下物理量的因果关系。

Method: 基于卷积信息论学习，将给定涡流快照分解为信息分量和残差分量，依据对未来目标变量（升力系数、尺度依赖能量传递）的贡献进行提取。

Result: 涡撞击前主要突出涡核；大规模分离后，剪切层也被捕捉到。升力相关结构与能量传递相关结构在撞击早期不同，撞击后趋于相似。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动方法能够选择性地提取与特定物理机制因果相关的重要流场结构，为从因果数据驱动视角研究瞬态气动流动提供支持。

Abstract: This study considers extracting causally important vortical structures from the extreme vortex gust-airfoil interaction at a chord-based Reynolds number of $5000$. This extraction is achieved by decomposing a given vortical flow snapshot into its informative and residual components based on the contribution to an arbitrary future target variable with convolutional information-theoretic learning. For the current vortex-airfoil interactions that exhibit transient and multiscale flow characteristics, we first examine the important vortical structures with respect to a future lift coefficient. While the vortex cores are primarily highlighted before vortex impingement, the emerging shear layers are additionally captured after the massive separation, which is evident from a comparison to an instantaneous force-element analysis. We further take the scale-dependent energy transfer as a future variable of interest to examine its impact on the extracted informative structures compared to the lift-associated structures. They are distinct from the lift-based structures in the early stage of the gust encounter yet become similar after impingement, revealing an analogy between informative structures across different transient aerodynamic mechanisms. The present data-driven approach selectively extracts the specific important flow structures responsible for the physics of interest, which can support studying a range of transient aerodynamic flows from the causal, data-driven perspective.

</details>


### [168] [Prediction of Spherical Bubble-Chain-Induced Liquid Flow through Far-Wake Superposition Based on Bubble-Chain Hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26468)
*Satoi Suzuki,Hiroaki Kusuno,Toshiyuki Sanada*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过高精度嵌入边界模拟研究了清洁球形气泡链中的水动力学相互作用，发现气泡诱导的液体流动并非由分散宽度决定，而是由气泡轨迹决定的远尾流空间分布主导。基于此开发了降阶模型，成功再现了实验中观察到的近乎均匀的向上液体流动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验观察到清洁球形气泡链会产生近乎均匀的向上液体流动，但其物理起源尚不清楚。

Method: 采用高精度嵌入边界模拟解析界面边界层并捕捉多气泡长程相互作用，量化气泡链中水动力学相互作用的演化，识别气泡诱导流动可表示为孤立气泡远尾流叠加的条件，并基于此开发降阶模型。

Result: 降阶模型成功复现了实验中观察到的近乎均匀的向上液体流动。与均匀分散排列对比表明，只有实验观测的气泡轨迹才能再现均匀流动，而均匀分散排列产生中心峰值速度分布。

Conclusion: 液体流动并非由分散宽度决定，而是由气泡轨迹决定的远尾流空间分布主导。本研究建立了气泡间水动力学相互作用、气泡分散与气泡诱导液体流动之间的物理框架，并提供了预测气泡链液体流动的降阶模型。

Abstract: Recent experimental observations revealed that clean spherical bubble chains generate a nearly uniform upward liquid flow. The physical origin of this liquid flow, however, remains unclear. Hydrodynamic interactions in aligned bubble chains were therefore investigated using high-accuracy embedded-boundary simulations that resolve the interfacial boundary layer while capturing long-range interactions among multiple clean spherical bubbles. The simulations were used to quantify the evolution of hydrodynamic interactions within bubble chains and to identify the conditions under which bubble-induced liquid flow can be represented by the superposition of isolated-bubble far wakes. Based on these findings, a reduced-order model was developed and applied to experimentally measured bubble trajectories. The model successfully reproduced the nearly uniform upward liquid flow observed in the experiments. To clarify the role of bubble dispersion, the predictions were compared with those for a uniformly dispersed bubble arrangement having the same overall dispersion width. While the uniformly dispersed arrangement produced a center-peaked velocity distribution, only the experimentally observed bubble trajectories reproduced the nearly uniform upward liquid flow. These results demonstrate that the liquid flow is governed not simply by the dispersion width but by the bubble trajectories that establish the spatial distribution of far wakes. The present study provides a physical framework linking hydrodynamic interactions among bubbles, bubble dispersion, and bubble-induced liquid flow, together with a reduced-order model for predicting the liquid flow generated by bubble chains.

</details>


### [169] [Rotational equivariance and locality in data-driven subgrid-scale closures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26850)
*Ryley McConkey,Julia Balla,Elyssa Hofgard,Tess Smidt,Abigail Bodner*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究旋转等变性作为归纳偏置在大涡模拟亚网格尺度闭合模型中的作用，发现等变性能够提升模型精度、参数效率和泛化能力，尤其在非局部架构中效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索旋转等变性作为归纳偏置是否能够提升基于数据的亚网格尺度闭合模型在精度、参数效率和泛化能力上的表现，特别是在实际滤波比条件下的表现。

Method: 在湍流槽道流中，对比数据增强的非等变架构与具有等变性归纳偏置的架构，分别考察逐点和非局部两种模型类别，在匹配参数量的条件下评估时空、各向异性和雷诺数泛化能力。

Result: 非增强模型能从湍流数据中学习到小幅度的等变性；等变非局部架构在所有泛化测试中相关系数最高，参数量约为非等变模型的一半；逐点架构未能超越Clark解析基线；等变性收益随感受野增大而增加。

Conclusion: 旋转等变性和非局部性对于亚网格尺度闭合任务均是有用的归纳偏置，在合理的数据规模、参数量和滤波尺寸下，等变性能够带来实质性的性能提升。

Abstract: Data-driven subgrid-scale closures for large eddy simulation are of significant interest in many engineering and geoscience applications. In this context, several important questions remain about the role of rotational equivariance as an inductive bias for learned tensorial mappings. We investigate whether equivariance improves accuracy, parameter efficiency, and generalization for subgrid-scale modelling at realistic filter ratios. For turbulent channel flow, we compare data-augmented non-equivariant architectures to those with equivariance as an inductive bias. We compare both pointwise and nonlocal versions of these two model classes. All models are evaluated at matched parameter counts across spatiotemporal, anisotropy, and Reynolds number generalization. We show that non-augmented models learn a small degree of equivariance directly from turbulence data, especially when that data is more isotropic. The equivariant nonlocal architecture attains the highest correlation coefficient on every generalization test at approximately half the parameter count of its non-equivariant counterpart, while the pointwise architectures do not improve on the analytical Clark baseline. Additionally, the equivariant model is more data-efficient than a non-equivariant model. The benefit of equivariance grows with the receptive field of the model, indicating that equivariance and nonlocality are both useful for the subgrid-scale closure task at realistic dataset size, parameter counts, and filter size.

</details>


### [170] [Disentangling intermittent flow structure contributions to anomalous scaling and multifractality in turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26896)
*Ritwik Mukherjee,Siddhartha Mukherjee*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过涡量阈值滤波和Biot-Savart逆变换方法，从湍流中分离间歇性结构的影响，发现去除极端涡量后能量谱标度保持但瓶颈变平，结构函数标度趋向Kolmogorov值，多分形性减弱，揭示了间歇性结构对湍流标度律和奇异性指数的选择性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流中的间歇性表现为剧烈涡旋和耗散尖峰，破坏了Kolmogorov的自相似理论，导致异常标度和多分形性，但间歇性流结构如何影响这些不同测量指标的定量关系尚不清楚。

Method: 采用涡量阈值滤波方法，对涡量场进行阈值截断，再通过Biot-Savart定律逆变换生成滤波后的速度场，从而分离间歇性结构对速度场的影响。

Result: 去除极端涡量贡献后：(1)能量谱标度保持不变，但瓶颈效应被展平；(2)结构函数标度趋向Kolmogorov值，横向指数更快恢复；(3)多分形性减弱，粗糙度奇异性指数范围缩小；(4)残余场呈现更强的多分形性，但其结构开始偏离湍流骨架；(5)涡拉伸和应变自放大效应被量化。

Conclusion: Biot-Savart方法能够选择性地从湍流中去除间歇性效应及其标度律影响，揭示了间歇性结构对湍流统计特性的定量贡献。

Abstract: Intermittency in turbulence manifests as intense vortices and sharp peaks of dissipation. Causing the breakdown of Kolmogorov's simple self-similar theory, it leads to anomalous scaling, multifractality and so far remains beyond the scope of a complete theoretical description. How intermittent flow structures influence these different measurements is not known quantitatively. With a simple filtering procedure-thresholding vorticity and inverting the Biot-Savart law to generate filtered velocity fields-we show the effects of intermittent flow structures can be disentangled. As extreme vorticity contributions to the velocity field are filtered out, the energy spectrum scaling persists, while the bottleneck is flattened, and structure function scalings tend towards their Kolmogorov values. The approach is more rapid for transverse exponents, revealing the selective importance of intensely swirling flow regions. Similarly, the extent of multifractality reduces as intermittency is filtered, shrinking the range of roughness singularity exponents. The residual fields are curiously more multifractal, but their structure begins to break away from an underlying turbulence skeleton. The effects on vortex stretching and strain self-amplification are quantified. Our work shows that a Biot-Savart approach can selectively remove the effects of intermittency from turbulence, and hence from its scalings.

</details>


### [171] [Enhancing hydrogen production in alkaline electrolyzers by using an ultra-fast kinetics surfactant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26750)
*D. Fernández-Martínez,G. Martín-Zazo,A. Rubio-González,J. L. Serrano-Marcos,J. M. Montanero,F. J. Perosanz,E. J. Vega*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了超快动力学表面活性剂Surfynol 465对水电解性能的影响。高速光学诊断表明，Surfynol 465可将气泡停留时间降低一个数量级，并阻止大于200μm的大气泡生长。在阴离子交换膜电解槽测试中，添加Surfynol 465使电池过电位降低140-150 mV，电流密度从0.13提升至0.37 A/cm²（2 V时），在一周测试中平均功率提升40%。下游腐蚀分析显示其效果与合金类型相关：可减少不锈钢和黄铜的腐蚀，但加速碳钢的腐蚀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究表面活性剂吸附动力学对水电解性能的影响，探索通过快速气泡脱离来提升电解效率的策略。

Method: 使用超快动力学表面活性剂Surfynol 465，结合高速光学诊断技术观测气泡行为，并在阴离子交换膜电解槽（AEMEL）测试平台进行性能验证和下游腐蚀分析。

Result: Surfynol 465使气泡停留时间减少一个数量级，阻止大气泡生成；过电位降低140-150 mV，电流密度提升近2倍（0.13→0.37 A/cm²），一周测试平均功率提升40%；减少不锈钢和黄铜腐蚀，但加速碳钢腐蚀。

Conclusion: 超快动力学表面活性剂可显著提升水电解性能，但其对材料腐蚀的影响具有合金依赖性，需根据具体材料选择使用。

Abstract: We study the effect of surfactant adsorption kinetics on water electrolysis performance using Surfynol 465, an ultrafast-kinetics surfactant. High-speed optical diagnostics reveal that Surfynol 465 reduces bubble residence time by an order of magnitude. Compared to the slower surfactant Triton X-100 and the surfactant-free baseline, it also prevents the growth of large bubbles ($>200\ μ$m). We validated these results on an anion-exchange membrane electrolyzer (AEMEL) test bench. Adding Surfynol 465 to the $1\text{ M KOH}$ electrolyte decreases cell overpotential by 140--150 mV. It nearly triples the current density (from 0.13 to 0.37 A/cm$^2$ at 2 V) and increases average power by 40\% during a week-long test. Furthermore, downstream corrosion analyses reveal a strong alloy-dependent response. The surfactant reduces degradation in stainless steel and brass but accelerates corrosion in carbon steel.

</details>


### [172] [Can we live Danckwerts' dream? Mixing Analysis in a Baffled Stirred Tank Reactor Based on 4D-Particle Tracking Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26813)
*Eike Steuwe,Thanh Tung Thai,Christian Weiland,Anna Klünker,Jan Hendrick Nissen,Michael Schlüter,Kathrin Padberg-Gehle,Alexandra von Kameke*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究利用四维粒子追踪测速技术，在3升搅拌槽反应器中追踪了多达40,000个示踪粒子的轨迹，揭示了五个影响物质分布的输运屏障隔室，实现了Danckwerts和Levenspiel的思想实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统搅拌槽反应器中的混合动力学缺乏高时空分辨率的实验数据，尤其是拉格朗日视角下的流体微团行为尚未被充分理解，这限制了化学和生化过程的优化。

Method: 采用时间分辨四维粒子追踪测速技术，在3升STR中（配备双Rushton涡轮和三个挡板）追踪多达40,000个示踪粒子，获取完整的拉格朗日轨迹数据，并应用基于网络的流形分析方法识别相干流体隔室。

Result: 成功揭示了各向异性的混合特性，识别出五个具有强内部混合但弱交换的相干流体隔室，这些隔室作为输运屏障对底物分布有显著影响。

Conclusion: 该方法扩展了Danckwerts和Levenspiel的思想实验，为化学和生化反应器的评估与优化提供了新的实验参考和数据分析手段。

Abstract: We present an experimental investigation of mixing dynamics within a laboratory-scale 3-liter stirred tank reactor (STR) equipped with two Rushton turbines and three baffles. Using time-resolved, four-dimensional particle tracking velocimetry, we successfully capture trajectories of up to 40,000 tracer particles in the full reactor volume despite obstructions by stirrer and baffles, providing unprecedented time-resolved flow and mixing information. From these Lagrangian data, we analyze velocities, accelerations, and spatial dispersion, revealing anisotropic mixing. By utilizing novel network-based analysis methods on the experimental particle trajectories, we identify coherent fluid compartments that exhibit strong internal mixing but weak exchange with neighboring compartments. We uncover five distinct compartments acting as transport barriers, which have a high impact on substrate distribution in chemical and biochemical processes. Our approach thus realizes and extends early thought experiments from Danckwerts and Levenspiel by providing detailed insight into the behavior of single fluid parcels and Lagrangian mixing withing chemical and biochemical reactors, offering a valuable approach for evaluation and optimization of chemical and biochemical processes. The trajectory data are made freely available to serve as an experimental reference for further research.

</details>


### [173] [Learning Backward Transport for Source Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26892)
*Maurizio Carbone,Lorenzo Piro*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Schrödinger桥的化学源定位方法，利用被动示踪物后向传播子，将浓度检测解释为连接源与检测点的路径证据，通过Langevin动力学采样候选排放位置，并在二维湍流中验证了该方法优于经典策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的化学源定位方法在复杂流动环境（如湍流）中性能有限，缺乏统一的物理原理来整合化学趋向性和cast-and-surge等不同搜索行为。

Method: 利用浓度场与拉格朗日示踪轨迹之间的对偶性，将浓度检测解释为连接源与检测点的路径证据；基于Schrödinger桥框架，结合被动示踪物的后向传播子，通过Langevin动力学采样候选排放位置；学习一个伽利略不变的传播子用于回溯。

Result: 在二维湍流嗅觉搜索任务中，所提出的回溯框架在不同风况下均优于经典搜索策略，且仅需一个单一的伽利略不变传播子。

Conclusion: 化学趋向性和cast-and-surge行为可以从统一的输运原理中涌现，Schrödinger桥框架为复杂流动中的源定位提供了一种高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: We address the problem of locating a chemical source in a flow. Based on the duality between the concentration field and Lagrangian tracer trajectories, we interpret concentration detections as evidence of paths connecting the source to the detection points. This Schrödinger bridge formulation between plausible emission positions and detection points leverages the backward propagator of passive tracers to frame source localization as the sampling of candidate emission locations via Langevin dynamics. The associated drift reveals classical chemotaxis and cast-and-surge as complementary behaviors emerging from a single transport-based principle. Applied to olfactory search in two-dimensional turbulence, the proposed backtracking framework outperforms classical strategies across varying wind regimes using a single, Galilean-invariant learned propagator.

</details>


### [174] [A New Paradigm for 3D Turbomachinery Design: Generative Diffusion Model Based Framework with Direct Geometry Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27093)
*Yingfan Geng,Jinhong Wang,Lazaros Papachristodoulou,Sibo Cheng,Teng Cao*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于去噪扩散模型的涡轮机械3D逆设计方法，以离心压缩机为例，通过指定设计条件和目标性能直接生成三维叶片几何形状。该方法实现了高达99%的求解精度，不可行设计低于1%，并能有效探索设计空间提供多样化解集，且首次直接在3D叶片几何坐标上训练而非参数化表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡轮机械的气动设计对能源系统性能至关重要，但由于复杂的非线性流动物理和多目标设计折衷，传统正向设计方法难以高效求解。逆向设计需要从目标性能反推几何形状，需要一种高精度、高多样性的生成式方法。

Method: 构建了以去噪扩散模型为核心的设计框架，直接以3D叶片几何坐标作为训练数据而非参数化表示，通过指定设计条件（质量流量、转速）和目标性能（压比、效率）作为输入条件，训练模型学习从条件到几何形状的生成映射。

Result: 扩散模型实现了高达99%的求解精度，不可行设计比例低于1%。通过比较扩散模型生成的解集与物理参数直接采样的解集分布，定量验证了模型生成的解具有良好的多样性。

Conclusion: 扩散模型在涡轮机械3D逆设计中表现出优异性能，能够直接生成满足目标性能的高精度几何解，同时保持解集的多样性。直接基于3D坐标学习的方法证明了可行性，为该领域提供了一个高度灵活的新框架。

Abstract: The aerodynamic design of turbomachinery is critical to the performance of the overall energy system, yet it is challenging due to the complex non-linear flow physics and the presence of multiple-target design compromises. Denoising diffusion model, as one of the leading approaches in generative machine learning, has shown its advantages of high design solution accuracy and diversity in many engineering applications. In this study, we bring it to the 3D inverse design problem of turbomachinery, using centrifugal compressors as a classic representative, to demonstrate the new methodology for complex geometry designs. A diffusion model-centred design framework has been developed in this study. By specifying the desired design condition (mass flow rate and rotational speed) and targeted performance (pressure ratio and efficiency), the trained diffusion model returns directly the 3D compressor geometry that satisfies the condition inputs. Compared to traditional deterministic forward design approaches, the proposed method not only generates accurate geometry solutions to inverse design problems, but also enables effective exploration of the entire design space, providing a diverse set of candidate solutions. In addition, this paper presents the first study to directly train on 3D blade geometry coordinates rather than parametrised representations, demonstrating the feasibility of coordinate-based learning while enabling a highly flexible framework applicable to a wide range of designs. The trained diffusion model achieves excellent design capability, with solution accuracy up to 99% and unfeasible designs less than 1%. Furthermore, the solution diversity of the trained diffusion model is also quantitatively verified by means of comparing the distribution of the solution sets generated from the diffusion model and from direct sampling of physical parameters.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [A Riemann-Roch theorem for Frobenius quotients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26176)
*Matthew Dupraz,Andreas Gross,Leonid Monin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文构造了交换 Frobenius 环 $K_f$ 和 $A_g$，分别作为移位算子代数与微分算子代数关于多项式零化子的商，用以模拟光滑完全簇的数值 $K$-环和 Chow 环。研究发现当多项式满足 Hirzebruch-Riemann-Roch 定理的组合类比时，存在类似 Chern 特征的同构 $\mathbb{Q} K_f \cong \mathbb{Q} A_g$，此时 $K_f$ 具有 $\lambda$-环、Chern 类、行列式、Serre 对偶等结构，并满足 Grothendieck-Riemann-Roch 定理的组合类比。文章还提供了大量应用，包括计算拟阵 Snapper 多项式的新公式、证明 $K_f$ 的对偶类与拟阵及线性多面体族的对偶类一致、将环面簇丛的 $K$-环实现为 Frobenius 商，以及研究 Ehrhart 扇的 $K$-环和例外同构等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为光滑完全簇的数值 $K$-环和 Chow 环构造一个纯代数的组合模型，使得经典 Riemann-Roch 理论中的核心结构（Chern 特征、Todd 类、Serre 对偶、Grothendieck-Riemann-Roch 定理等）在该模型中都有自然的组合对应物。

Method: 构造两类交换 Frobenius 环：离散型 $K_f$（移位算子代数商）和连续型 $A_g$（微分算子代数商），通过多项式 $f,g$ 的零化子定义；研究它们之间的同构条件，引入组合 Hirzebruch-Riemann-Roch 定理（存在可逆元 td 使得 $f = \mathrm{td} \cdot g$）；证明在该条件下 $K_f$ 具有 $\lambda$-环、Chern 类、行列式、Serre 对偶等结构，并建立函子性和 Grothendieck-Riemann-Roch 定理的组合类比；进一步研究更一般的幂级数作用下的同构族。

Result: 1) 建立了 $\mathbb{Q} K_f \cong \mathbb{Q} A_g$ 的同构（Chern 特征类比）当且仅当存在 Todd 类 td 满足 $f = \mathrm{td} \cdot g$；2) 证明在此条件下 $K_f$ 是 $\lambda$-环，具有 Chern 类、行列式，满足 Serre 对偶；3) 建立了 Grothendieck-Riemann-Roch 定理的组合类比；4) 给出了计算拟阵 Snapper 多项式的新公式；5) 证明了 $K_f$ 的对偶类与拟阵及线性多面体族的对偶类一致；6) 将环面簇丛的 $K$-环实现为 Frobenius 商；7) 研究了 Ehrhart 扇的 $K$-环和例外同构。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了光滑完全簇 $K$-理论和 Chow 理论的一个纯代数组合模型，将 Riemann-Roch 理论的核心定理翻译为多项式环上的代数条件，并通过大量例子和跨领域应用（拟阵、多面体、环面簇、Ehrhart 理论等）展示了该模型的广泛适用性和深刻联系。

Abstract: We construct two families of commutative Frobenius rings: discrete and continuous Frobenius quotients $K_f$, resp. $A_g$, which are defined as the quotients of shift, resp. differential operator algebras by the annihilator of a polynomial. These constructions model the numerical $K$-rings and Chow rings of smooth complete varieties. We show that there is a naturally defined isomorphism $\mathbb{Q} K_f \cong \mathbb{Q} A_g$ playing the role of the Chern character, precisely when the polynomials satisfy a combinatorial analogue of the Hirzebruch-Riemann-Roch theorem, which states that there exists an invertible element $\mathrm{td} \in \mathbb{Q} A_g^\times$, called the Todd class, such that $f = \mathrm{td} \cdot g$. In this case we show that $K_f$ carries all the structure needed to make it a suitable model for $K$-rings of complete smooth varieties: $K_f$ is a $λ$-ring, has well-defined Chern classes, determinants, and satisfies a combinatorial analogue of Serre duality. We further show that the construction is functorial and obtain a combinatorial analogue of the Grothendieck-Riemann-Roch theorem. Lastly, we investigate the existence of larger families of isomorphisms between Frobenius quotients defined by the action of a power series on a generating set, such as the truncated Chern character which yields an integral isomorphism $K_f \cong A_g$.
  We provide numerous examples and applications: we give a new formula for computing Snapper polynomials of matroids, show that the dualizing class of $K_f$ coincides with dualizing classes of matroids and linear families of polytopes, realize $K$-rings of toric variety bundles as Frobenius quotients, and study $K$-rings of Ehrhart fans as well as exceptional isomorphisms in this setting.

</details>


### [176] [Algebraic vs. analytic: a survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26190)
*Aravind Asok,Tom Bachmann,Michael J. Hopkins*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文探讨复代数簇之间的解析映射何时具有代数表示，特别关注非紧情形下该问题与Hodge型猜想的联系，具体表现为仿射簇上的全纯向量丛何时具有代数结构，以及映射的同伦类何时具有代数代表元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是解决解析对象与代数对象之间的表示性问题：给定一对复代数簇，是否每个解析映射都存在代数代表元？这一问题在非紧情形下尤为有趣且与Hodge型猜想紧密相关。

Method: 从多个视角（可能包括Hodge理论、代数几何、解析几何等）讨论该问题的具体表现形式，包括仿射簇上全纯向量丛的代数化问题，以及映射同伦类的代数代表元问题。

Result: 指出了两类具体问题：1) 仿射簇上哪些全纯向量丛可赋予代数结构；2) 哪些映射的同伦类存在代数代表元。

Conclusion: 该问题的研究与非紧复代数簇的几何性质密切相关，是连接解析几何与代数几何、Hodge猜想的重要桥梁。

Abstract: Given a pair of complex algebraic varieties, we can ask: does every analytic map admit an algebraic representative? Such questions are most interesting when the source and target are non-compact varieties, in which case the problems are closely linked with Hodge-type conjectures. Concrete avatars of this kind of problem, which we discuss from several points of view, include: which holomorphic vector bundles on an affine variety admit an algebraic structure, and which homotopy classes of maps admit algebraic representatives?

</details>


### [177] [On the Betti numbers, Poincaré polynomials, and Euler characteristics of $\overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26755)
*Giordano Cotti*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了稳定n点有理曲线Deligne-Mumford模空间$\overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n}$的Poincaré多项式、Betti数和Euler示性数。给出了Aluffi-Marcolli-Nascimento和Eur-Ferroni-Matherne-Pagaria-Vecchi两个近期闭公式的初等推导，表明它们已隐含于Getzler和Manin的生成级数结果中。此外，利用Lambert W函数和完全Bell多项式获得了Euler示性数的Hessenberg行列式公式、新线性递推关系及完整渐近展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视$\overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n}$的拓扑不变量，提供更初等的推导方法，并揭示不同公式之间的内在联系。同时，获得Euler示性数的新表示和更精细的渐近展开。

Method: 通过生成函数的初等操作、二项式级数和Stirling数标准恒等式，从Getzler-Manin生成级数结果中提取Poincaré多项式公式。利用Lambert W函数的Taylor展开和完全Bell多项式研究Euler示性数，推导Hessenberg行列式公式和新递推关系。

Result: 得到了两个已知Poincaré多项式公式的初等推导，发现了新的线性递推关系，获得了Betti数的两个未记录新公式，以及Euler示性数的Hessenberg行列式公式、新线性递推和完整渐近展开。

Conclusion: 所有公式均可统一从经典生成函数结果中导出，Bell多项式方法提供了研究Euler示性数的新视角，并精化了Manin-Zagier的渐近估计。

Abstract: In this paper, we revisit the Poincaré polynomials, Betti numbers, and Euler characteristics of the Deligne-Mumford moduli spaces $\overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n}$ of stable $n$-pointed rational curves. We give elementary derivations of two recent closed formulas for their Poincaré polynomials, due respectively to Aluffi-Marcolli-Nascimento (arXiv:2406.13095) and to Eur-Ferroni-Matherne-Pagaria-Vecchi (arXiv:2504.16776). Our approach shows that both formulas are already implicit in the generating-series results of Getzler and Manin, and can be extracted from them by elementary manipulations of generating functions, the binomial series, and standard identities for Stirling numbers. Beyond these new derivations, the same method also yields new linear recurrence relations for refined invariants associated with these Poincaré polynomials, namely distinguished summands and a bivariate refinement. As a further consequence, we obtain two additional formulas for the Betti numbers, not previously recorded in this form.
  We also study the Euler characteristics $χ(\overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n})$. Using the Taylor expansion of a suitable branch of the Lambert $W$-function, we show that their sequence is obtained by evaluating complete Bell polynomials at an explicit auxiliary integer sequence. This Bell-polynomial representation yields Hessenberg determinantal formulas and a new linear recursion, distinct from the well-known quadratic Keel-Manin recursion. It also provides an explicit extraction of the Euler characteristics from the Lambert $W$-function expression considered by Aluffi-Marcolli-Nascimento. Finally, we refine the Manin-Zagier asymptotic estimate for these Euler characteristics by computing the full asymptotic expansion.

</details>


### [178] [Higher dimensional dominoes in de Rham-Witt cohomology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26323)
*Yuanning Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文对特征p光滑真簇的de Rham-Witt上同调中"多米诺骨牌"（domino）结构进行了系统分类：完整分类了二维多米诺，建立了任意维度的正规形式，并将其与两个幺幂群（形式群与完美群）的同源划分联系起来。在二维情形中，从Mazur-Ogus簇的晶体上同调恢复了多米诺，计算了两个超奇异阿贝尔簇族的多米诺，并用a-数界定了p-主Brauer群的指数，回答了Grammatica-Skorobogatov-Yang的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: de Rham-Witt上同调中的"多米诺"承载了斜率谱序列的非零微分，但在一维以上未被分类，限制了对其结构的理解。同时，Grammatica-Skorobogatov-Yang提出了关于p-主Brauer群指数的具体问题，需要从多米诺角度给出回答。

Method: 采用分类法与结构分析：对二维多米诺进行完整分类；建立任意维度多米诺的正规形式；通过构造形式幺幂群和完美幺幂群并证明其同源划分一致来建立代数结构联系；在二维情形中从晶体上同调恢复Mazur-Ogus簇的多米诺，并对超奇异阿贝尔簇进行计算。

Result: 完成了二维多米诺的完整分类；建立了任意维度多米诺的正规形式；证明了两个幺幂群的同源划分一致；在二维情形中从晶体上同调恢复了Mazur-Ogus簇的多米诺；计算了两个超奇异阿贝尔簇族的多米诺；用a-数给出了p-主Brauer群指数的上界。

Conclusion: 回答了Grammatica-Skorobogatov-Yang关于p-主Brauer群指数的问题，为de Rham-Witt上同调中多米诺结构提供了系统的分类与代数刻画。

Abstract: The de Rham-Witt cohomology of a smooth proper variety in characteristic $p$ contains a canonical piece called the domino, which is not finitely generated over the Witt vectors and carries the nonzero differentials of the slope spectral sequence. Beyond dimension one, dominoes were unclassified. We classify the two-dimensional ones and put a domino of any dimension into a normal form. To each domino we attach two unipotent groups, one formal and one perfect, and prove that their isogeny partitions agree. In degree two we recover the domino of a Mazur-Ogus variety from its crystalline cohomology, compute it for two families of supersingular abelian varieties, and bound the exponent of the $p$-primary Brauer group in terms of the $a$-number, for every prime $p$. This answers a question of Grammatica-Skorobogatov-Yang.

</details>


### [179] [Twelve common flex lines in a general pencil of cubics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26396)
*Jihao Liu,Yanze Wang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一般复平面三次曲线束恰好有12条公共的拐线，回答了Ciliberto、Miranda和Roé的问题。该结果使用了生成式AI（ChatGPT 5.5 Pro和Danus系统）获得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Ciliberto、Miranda和Roé提出的关于平面三次曲线束公共拐线数量的问题。

Method: 使用生成式人工智能工具（ChatGPT 5.5 Pro和Danus系统）辅助证明。

Result: 证明了一般复平面三次曲线束恰好有12条公共拐线。

Conclusion: 回答了Ciliberto、Miranda和Roé的公开问题，并展示了生成式AI在代数几何研究中的潜在应用。

Abstract: We prove that a general pencil of plane cubics over $\mathbb C$ has exactly $12$ common flex lines. This answers a question of Ciliberto, Miranda, and Roé. The main result of this paper was obtained using generative AI, particularly ChatGPT 5.5 Pro and the Danus system.

</details>


### [180] [An atomic criterion for irrationality without quantum computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26718)
*Vladimiro Benedetti,Aideen Fay,Jérémy Guéré,Laurent Manivel,Nicolas Perrin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子上同调与单值表示不可约性的新判据，无需计算原子即可推导出一般成员的 irrationality，并将其应用于一般三次四重、Gushel-Mukai 四重以及 Kǘchle (c5) 型四重，证明了后者的 irrationality。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Katzarkov-Kontsevich-Pantev-Yu 引入的双有理不变量可用于获得 varieties 的非有理性质，但计算原子本身可能较为困难。本文旨在寻找一个更易操作的判据，通过较弱的条件（单值等变性等）直接判定 irrationality。

Method: 利用量子乘法的单值等变性和单值表示的不可约性，在特定上同调条件下，从原子理论中推导出一般成员的 irrationality，而无需实际计算原子。

Result: 该判据成功应用于一般三次四重、Gushel-Mukai 四重以及 Kǘchle (c5) 型四重。前两者的非有理性已知，后者（指数为1的 Fano 流形）的非有理性为新结果。

Conclusion: 本文建立的判据简化了某些双有理不变量的验证过程，拓展了 irrationality 结果的适用范围，特别是对指数为1的 Fano 四重流形。

Abstract: The birational invariants introduced by Katzarkov-Kontsevich-Pantev-Yu allows one to obtain irrationality results for varieties whose quantum cohomology is well-behaved. We observe that under certain cohomological conditions, we can deduce irrationality of a very general member from the theory of atoms without actually computing them, using only monodromy equivariance of quantum multiplication and irreducibility of the monodromy representation. Our criterion applies to the very general cubic and Gushel-Mukai fourfolds, whose irrationalities were already known, but also to the very general K{ü}chle fourfold of type (c5), which is a Fano manifold of index one.

</details>


### [181] [Image Recognition via Vaisman--Neifeld's Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26749)
*N. C. Combe,H. K. Nencka*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合几何积分与拓扑分析（基于Vaisman和Neifeld框架）的新方法，通过对配置空间进行不变叶理和矩映射的几何分解，解决欠定反问题中的内在歧义，构建鲁棒且计算可行的图像与结构重建框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从不完整数据中重建隐藏结构时，欠定反问题存在固有的歧义性，现有方法缺乏统一的几何与拓扑视角来处理这种不确定性。

Method: 将配置空间精炼几何分解为不变叶理和矩映射，融合Vaisman的对称性分析与Neifeld的分析方法论，构建统一的抗噪声重建框架。

Result: 建立了计算可行且抗噪声的重建框架，能够统一处理成像与结构分析中的反问题。

Conclusion: 该方法在跨科学领域具有广泛适用性，揭示了几何与拓扑在反问题求解中的协同作用。

Abstract: We introduce a new approach to the reconstruction of hidden structures from incomplete data, unifying techniques from geometric integration and topological analysis within the frameworks of Vaisman and Neifeld. Our method employs a refined geometric decomposition of configuration spaces into invariant foliations and moment maps, thereby addressing the intrinsic ambiguities of underdetermined inverse problems. By combining Vaisman's insights into symmetry with Neifeld's analytical methodologies, we establish a robust, noise-resistant framework that ensures computational tractability while providing a unified perspective on reconstruction in imaging and structural analysis. This approach enables applications across diverse scientific domains and highlights the interplay between geometry and topology in the solution of inverse problems.

</details>


### [182] [Fundamental group schemes of globally F-regular varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26785)
*Charles Vial*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在正特征代数闭域上，连通正规全局F-正则射影簇的拟挠子覆盖塔必然稳定，且其正则点的Nori基本群概形是有限的且线性可约的。线性可约性意味着任意拟挠子覆盖都是驯服的，而有限性则是Xu和Braun关于对数Fano复射影簇光滑点基本群有限性定理的正特征类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Xu和Braun关于复射影簇光滑点基本群有限性的结果推广到正特征情形，同时研究正特征代数几何中全局F-正则簇的基本群性质。

Method: 通过证明连通拟挠子覆盖塔的稳定性，结合Nori基本群概形的理论，利用正特征代数几何中的F-正则性条件进行分析。

Result: 连通正常全局F-正则射影簇的正则点Nori基本群概形是有限的且线性可约的，且拟挠子覆盖塔稳定。

Conclusion: 为正特征中F-正则簇的基本群理论提供了重要结果，建立了与复情形下对数Fano簇基本群有限性的正特征类比。

Abstract: We prove that every tower of connected quasi-torsor covers of a connected normal globally $F$-regular projective variety over an algebraically closed field of positive characteristic stabilizes, and that the Nori fundamental group scheme of its regular locus is finite and linearly reductive. The linear reductivity asserts that any quasi-torsor cover is tame, while the finiteness is a positive-characteristic analogue of theorems of Xu and Braun on the finiteness of the fundamental group of the smooth locus of a log Fano complex projective variety.

</details>


### [183] [A non-holomorphic P=W phenomenon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26806)
*Zili Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了三维孤立簇变体在不满足全秩假设下的P=W恒等式。在P侧，通过实解析几何分析构造了perverse截断；在W侧，构造了非正常代数态射并研究了常值层的导出推前在混合Hodge模导出范畴中的分解。有趣的是，在非全秩情形下，W侧的curious hard Lefschetz性质和P侧的相对hard Lefschetz性质均失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将P=W猜想推广到更一般的孤立簇变体，特别是去掉全秩假设并处理三维奇异情形，同时处理P侧拉格朗日纤维化非复代数这一技术困难。

Method: P侧：通过细致的实解析几何分析构造perverse截断；W侧：构造非正常代数态射，利用混合Hodge模导出范畴分析常值层沿此态射的导出推前分解。

Result: 在三维非全秩孤立簇变体上证明了P=W恒等式，并发现此时W侧的curious hard Lefschetz性质和P侧的相对hard Lefschetz性质均不成立。

Conclusion: P=W恒等式在更一般的非全秩三维孤立簇变体上成立，且其成立不依赖于经典的hard Lefschetz性质，表明该恒等式具有更广泛的适用性和深层几何意义。

Abstract: We prove the P=W identity for isolated cluster varieties of dimension 3 without the full rank hypothesis. These cluster varieties are generally singular, and the associated Lagrangian fibrations are not complex algebraic. On the P side, we construct the perverse truncation by a detailed analysis of the explicit real-analytic geometry of the Lagrangian fibration. On the W side, we construct a natural non-proper algebraic morphism from the cluster varieties and investigate the decomposition of the derived push-forward of the constant sheaf along this morphism within the derived category of mixed Hodge modules. In the P=W phenomenon for non-full rank isolated cluster varieties of dimension 3, both the curious hard Lefschetz property on the W side and the relative hard Lefschetz property on the P side fail.

</details>


### [184] [Splitting Brauer classes by genus one curves over number fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26915)
*Benjamin Antieau,Asher Auel,Federico Scavia*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明：在数域上，每个维数至少2的Severi-Brauer簇都包含一条扭椭圆正规曲线；因此每个数域上的Brauer类都可以被一条亏格1的曲线分裂。证明通过纤维化方法，展示了扭椭圆正规曲线的Hilbert概形的光滑紧化上的有理点在其Brauer-Manin集中稠密。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Severi-Brauer簇上是否存在扭椭圆正规曲线，以及Brauer类是否可被亏格1曲线分裂，这是算术几何中关于有理点和Brauer群结构的基础问题。

Method: 使用纤维化方法（fibration method），研究扭椭圆正规曲线的Hilbert概形的光滑紧化，证明其有理点在Brauer-Manin集中稠密。

Result: (1) 每个维数≥2的Severi-Brauer簇包含一条扭椭圆正规曲线；(2) 每个数域上的Brauer类可被某个亏格1曲线分裂。

Conclusion: 建立了Severi-Brauer簇与椭圆曲线之间的深刻联系，拓展了Brauer-Manin obstruction的研究工具，对理解数域上的有理点分布有重要意义。

Abstract: We prove that every Severi-Brauer variety of dimension at least two over a number field contains a twisted elliptic normal curve. Consequently, every Brauer class over a number field is split by a genus one curve. We prove this using the fibration method, by showing that the rational points on a smooth compactification of the Hilbert scheme of twisted elliptic normal curves are dense in its Brauer-Manin set.

</details>


### [185] [Removing the Torsion-free Hypothesis in a Positivity Theorem on Deligne-Mumford Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26989)
*Shengyu Hou,Jihao Liu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文去除了Casalaina-Martin和Zhjeqi一个正性定理中的无挠假设，回答了他们的一个问题。该结果是通过生成式AI（特别是ChatGPT 5.5 Pro和Danus系统）获得的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Casalaina-Martin和Zhjeqi提出的问题，即去除其正性定理中的无挠假设。

Method: 使用生成式AI（ChatGPT 5.5 Pro和Danus系统）辅助推导和证明。

Result: 成功去除了原定理中的无挠假设，推广了该正性定理。

Conclusion: 无挠假设对于该正性定理并非必要，且生成式AI在数学定理证明中具有辅助作用。

Abstract: We remove the torsion-free hypothesis from a positivity theorem of Casalaina-Martin and Zhjeqi, answering a question of them. The main result of this paper was obtained using generative AI, particularly ChatGPT 5.5 Pro and the Danus system.

</details>


### [186] [The finite degree formula for normalized volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27032)
*Zhiyu Liu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在有限满射态射下，klt奇点的归一化体积满足乘法公式：体积因子等于映射次数。证实了[LLX20, Zhu25, XZ26a]中的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在证明klt奇点在有限满射态射下的归一化体积与映射次数的关系，以验证前人的猜想。

Method: 通过分析有限满射态射 f 在klt奇点 (X,Δ_X) 和 (Y,Δ_Y) 之间的性质，利用 K_X+Δ_X = f^*(K_Y+Δ_Y) 的条件，推导出归一化体积的乘积关系。

Result: 证明了公式 vol̂(x, X, Δ_X) = deg(f) · vol̂(y, Y, Δ_Y) 成立。

Conclusion: 该结果证实了[LLX20, Zhu25, XZ26a]中的猜想，为klt奇点的体积理论研究提供了重要进展。

Abstract: Let $f\colon \big(x\in (X, Δ_X)\big)\to \big(y\in (Y, Δ_Y)\big)$ be a finite surjective morphism between klt singularities such that $K_X+Δ_X=f^*(K_Y+Δ_Y)$. We show that the normalized volumes satisfy \[\widehat{\mathrm{vol}}(x, X, Δ_X)=\mathrm{deg}(f)\cdot \widehat{\mathrm{vol}}(y, Y, Δ_Y).\] This proves a conjecture in [LLX20, Zhu25, XZ26a].

</details>


### [187] [On the boundedness of elliptic Calabi-Yau 4-folds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27048)
*Stefano Filipazzi,Fulin Xu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了非分裂型椭圆卡拉比-丘四维流形（即不与 $Y \times E$ 的商空间作双有理等价）属于有限代数族，特别地，当椭圆纤维化非等平凡时结论成立，并部分限定了卡拉比-丘三维流形的中贝蒂数有界性，同时研究了纤维化 $K$-平凡四维流形的指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一大类椭圆卡拉比-丘四维流形的有界性问题，从而在代数几何中推进对高维卡拉比-丘流形分类的经典问题。

Method: 通过区分椭圆卡拉比-丘四维流形是否双有理等价于 $Y \times E$ 的商空间（即是否分裂），对非分裂情形证明其属于有限代数族；对分裂情形则需额外条件（如纤维化非等平凡）以保证结论成立。同时通过分析纤维化 $K$-平凡四维流形的指数提供部分证据。

Result: 证明了非分裂椭圆卡拉比-丘四维流形属于有限代数族；当椭圆纤维化非等平凡时，结论对分裂情形也成立；给出了卡拉比-丘三维流形中贝蒂数有界性的部分证据。

Conclusion: 本文为一类椭圆卡拉比-丘四维流形的有界性分类奠定了重要基础，并暗示了更一般的有界性结果可能成立。

Abstract: In this work, we settle the boundedness of a vast class of elliptic Calabi-Yau 4-folds. In particular, we show that any elliptic Calabi-Yau 4-fold that is not crepant to the quotient of a product $Y \times E$, where $E$ is an elliptic curve and $Y$ a Calabi-Yau 3-fold, belongs to finitely many algebraic families. In particular, the statement applies whenever the elliptic fibration of the Calabi-Yau 4-fold is not isotrivial. We also provide partial evidence for the boundedness of the middle Betti number of Calabi-Yau 3-folds and study the index of fibered $K$-trivial 4-folds.

</details>
